# 决策记录 — Phase 2: Pure Pose Heatmap

### [2026-03-07 19:30] 决策 #1

**上下文**: Phase 1 (33 个实验) 已充分探索了基于 ViTPose visibility 向量的所有方向。最佳训练端改进仅 +1.4%（GiLt+PCFC）。用户指示放弃 visibility 方向，转向纯 pose heatmap + mmpose 更鲁棒模型。

**选择**: 从 SOLIDER 作者原始代码重新开始，纯 pose heatmap 方向
**理由**:
1. Visibility 向量不够可靠（与 AP 相关性仅 0.237）
2. PCFC alpha suppression 限制了所有后续改进
3. 纯 pose 热图更原始、更可靠、更多论文验证过有效性
4. 从干净代码开始避免 33 个实验的代码污染

**执行计划**:
1. 先跑 baseline 确认可复现（预期 mAP ~56.6%）
2. 选择并部署 mmpose 模型进行 pose heatmap 提取
3. 设计新的 heatmap 利用方式

### [2026-03-07 19:22] 决策 #2

**上下文**: 用户补充了多条重要指导：
1. 姿态模型可以参与训练（不限于离线热图）
2. 尽可能做训练侧创新（NFC/RR 等 test-time 方法不够公平）
3. 可以大胆修改 backbone 中间层
4. 数据必须从 log 文件读取，不能凭记忆

**选择**: 以训练侧创新为核心方向
**理由**:
1. NFC/Re-ranking 等 test-time 方法所有 SOTA 都可以用，不算公平的对比
2. 训练侧创新才是论文的核心贡献
3. 用户允许 pose 模型参与训练 + 修改中间层 → 创新空间大大增加
4. 可以考虑：在线 pose 特征注入、pose-guided attention、pose 结构约束等

**潜在技术方向**:
- 冻结的 pose 模型提取特征 → 通过 cross-attention 注入 Swin 中间层
- Pose heatmap 作为 spatial attention bias 直接修改 window attention
- Pose 骨骼结构约束 part features 之间的关系

### [2026-03-09 11:55] 决策 #3

**上下文**: exp001 (Pose Part Pooling with sigmoid) 完成。结果：mAP 57.1% (+0.5%), R1 66.7% (+0.2%)。有效但提升有限。关键发现：id_part 收敛极慢（最终仍在 2.0 vs id_global 0.2），说明 sigmoid 热图在 12×4 分辨率的 soft attention pooling 不够 discriminative。

**选项**:
  A. 改进 part pooling：使用 spatial softmax 代替 sigmoid，增强热图峰值对比度
  B. 放弃 part pooling，转向 pose heatmap 作为 attention bias 注入 Swin backbone
  C. 使用更高分辨率的中间层特征（stage2: 24×8 而非 stage3: 12×4）

**选择**: 先试 A（spatial softmax 改进），如果 id_part 收敛改善但最终结果仍有限，再转 B
**理由**:
1. exp001 证明 part pooling 方向有效（+0.5% mAP），但 id_part 是瓶颈
2. Spatial softmax 是最小改动，只改一行代码就能验证 "热图对比度不够" 的假设
3. 如果 id_part 收敛问题解决，part pooling 可能有更大提升空间
4. 如果 A 验证后仍不够，则 B 是完全不同的方向，有更大的创新性

**执行结果**: exp002 结果 mAP 57.2% vs exp001 57.1%，几乎无差异。id_part 训练中期收敛更快但最终效果相同。**结论：归一化方式不是瓶颈，转向方案 B。**

### [2026-03-09 14:13] 决策 #4

**上下文**: exp001 和 exp002 结果对比完成。两种归一化方式（sigmoid vs spatial_softmax）效果几乎一致。特征模式消融发现 part-only > concat > global，说明 part 特征有效但融合方式有问题。

**关键发现**:
1. 两种 normalization 最终 part-only mAP 都是 57.5%（+0.9% vs baseline）
2. Concat 融合反而比 part-only 差（1/N scaling 稀释信号）
3. id_part 收敛慢不是 normalization 的问题，而是 12×4 分辨率下 part 区分度本身有限

**选项**:
  A. 改进 part pooling 的融合方式（如 learnable weights, attention-based fusion）
  B. 转向 pose heatmap attention bias 注入 Swin backbone 中间层
  C. 提高 part 特征图分辨率（使用 stage2 特征 24×8）

**选择**: A — 改进特征融合方式。Part 特征已被证明有效（+0.9% mAP），但融合方式拖累了整体效果。这是最直接的改进方向。

**理由**:
1. Part-only 已经超 baseline 0.9%，说明 part 学到了有用信息
2. 当前 concat 的 1/N scaling 太朴素，直接稀释了 part 信号
3. 改进融合方式是低风险高回报：不需要改 backbone，只需修改测试时的特征组合
4. 如果简单的融合改进有效，可以作为消融实验的重要证据
5. B 和 C 是更大的改动，作为备选

**具体方案**: exp003 — 移除 1/N scaling，等权拼接 global + parts；或测试只用 part-only 作为最终特征

**执行结果**: exp003 在 ep60 终止，mAP 50.2%（-6.4% vs baseline）。降低 global loss weight 严重伤害 backbone 特征质量。Part 分类器虽学得更快（id_part 2.08 vs exp001 ~3.3），但池化的 backbone 特征变差了。**结论：global 和 part 是共生关系，不能通过削弱 global 来强化 part。**

### [2026-03-09 15:32] 决策 #5

**上下文**: exp001-003 完成。核心发现：
1. Part pooling 有效（+0.9% mAP with part-only feature）
2. 归一化方式（sigmoid vs spatial_softmax）无差异
3. 融合方式：part-only > concat（1/N scaling 有害）
4. 降低 global weight 反而伤害 part（因为 backbone 质量下降）
5. id_part 收敛极慢是核心瓶颈（id_part≈2.0 vs id_global≈0.2）

**关键问题**: 如何在不削弱 backbone 的前提下增强 part 学习？

**选项**:
  A. 独立 Part BN+分类器 + 更高 LR（加速 part 收敛，不改 global loss weight）
  B. 转向 Direction B：Pose heatmap 作为 attention bias 注入 Swin backbone 中间层（全新方向）
  C. Part feature 使用更高分辨率特征图（stage2: 24×8 而非 stage3: 12×4）
  D. 在 Part head 加入额外的 self-attention 层增强 part 特征表达
  E. 改进 Part 学习信号：per-part triplet loss (GiLt) + part-specific augmentation

**选择**: E — Per-part triplet loss (GiLt)
**理由**:
1. Phase 1 中 GiLt 已证明有效（+0.5% on top of PCFC），这次在 pure heatmap 框架下重试
2. 当前 part triplet 是"所有 part 特征拼起来做一个 triplet"，每个 part 没有独立的 hard positive/negative mining
3. Per-part triplet 让每个 part 独立学习判别性特征，直接解决 id_part 收敛慢的问题
4. 最小改动：只改 loss 计算方式，不改 backbone 或 part pooling 模块
5. 如果 GiLt 有效，可以作为消融实验的重要证据（"per-part triplet vs global triplet"）

**方案 B 作为备选**：如果 GiLt + part pooling 组合无法超过 +1.5% mAP，则转向全新的 backbone attention 方向。

**执行结果**: exp004 PFM 是中性结果。mAP 与 exp001 part-only 相同（57.5%），R1 反而下降 0.8%。PFM 加速收敛但不改善最终表征。**结论：不要在同一处重复使用 pose 信息（PFM+part pooling 是冗余的）。**

### [2026-03-09 17:52] 决策 #6

**上下文**: exp001-004 已探索了当前 part pooling 架构的多个变体：
- exp001/002: 不同热图归一化（sigmoid vs spatial_softmax）→ 无差异
- exp003: 改变 loss 权重 → 负面
- exp004: 加 PFM feature modulation → 中性

当前最佳：mAP 57.5% (part-only), R1 67.1% (+0.9%/+0.6% vs baseline)

**核心瓶颈**: id_part ≈ 2.0 无法进一步降低，part 特征质量受限于 12×4 分辨率。

**选项**:
  A. 使用 stage 2 特征 (24×8, 384ch) 做 part pooling — 4× spatial resolution
  B. Part diversity loss — 惩罚 part 特征间的相似度
  C. 转向 backbone attention 注入 — 修改 Swin 中间层
  D. Part-specific data augmentation — 基于 pose 的部位级数据增强
  E. Adaptive global-part fusion — 学习动态融合权重

**选择**: A — 使用 stage 2 高分辨率特征做 part pooling

**理由**:
1. 当前 12×4 分辨率对 5 个 part 来说太粗（每个 part 只能覆盖 2-3 个 spatial position）
2. Stage 2 (24×8 = 192 positions) 提供 4× 空间分辨率，pose heatmap attention 可以更精确
3. 384 channels 虽然比 768 少，但仍然有丰富的语义信息
4. 实验简单：只需改一下 part pooling 使用的特征图来源
5. 如果分辨率是瓶颈，这个实验会看到 id_part 明显改善

**风险**: Stage 2 特征可能不够 semantic（还没经过 stage 3 的进一步抽象）

**执行结果**: exp005 明确负面。ep40 mAP 仅 37.0%（baseline 56.6%）。id_part 到 ep49 才降到 4.70（exp001 同期 ~2.0）。**确认：Stage 2 特征语义不足以支撑 part-level identity 分类。** 更高空间分辨率无法补偿语义信息的缺失。

### [2026-03-09 19:10] 决策 #7

**上下文**: exp005 证明浅层（stage 2）特征不够 semantic。exp001-005 总结：
- Part pooling 方向的上限在 +0.9% mAP（part-only mode）
- Fusion 方式（concat_scaled vs equal_concat）对最终结果影响约 0.4%
- PFM 是冗余的；stage 2 太浅
- id_part 收敛始终慢于 id_global

**核心问题**：如何突破 +0.9% 的瓶颈？

**选项**:
  A. 改进 test-time 融合（L2-norm concat）— 可能挤出 0.2-0.5%，但不需要训练
  B. 转向 backbone attention 注入 — pose 信息参与特征形成过程
  C. 多尺度 part pooling — stage 2 spatial + stage 3 semantic 的融合
  D. Part feature diversity loss — 惩罚 part 间的冗余
  E. Pose-guided token selection — 剔除无关 token 提高效率

**选择**: 先做 A（test-time L2-norm fusion，零成本验证），然后转 B（backbone attention）

**理由**:
1. A 不需要训练，5 分钟可验证，如果能把 +0.9% 提升到 +1.2%，对论文有价值
2. B 是全新方向，改变特征形成过程本身，可能突破当前 part pooling 的上限
3. C-E 仍在 part pooling 框架内优化，上限有限
4. B 的创新性更好（"pose-conditioned attention" vs "better pooling"），更适合论文

**执行结果**:
- exp006 (A): L2-norm concat 57.4% vs concat 57.2%，小改进但仍不如 part-only (57.5%)。融合方向上限已到。
- **exp007 (B): PSG backbone injection → mAP 58.3%, R1 67.9%。Phase 2 最佳结果！+1.7% mAP, +1.4% R1。超过 Phase 1 最佳 (58.0%/68.0%)。**

### [2026-03-09 21:25] 决策 #8

**上下文**: exp007 PSG 取得突破性结果 (58.3%/67.9%)。关键发现：
1. Backbone-level pose injection (+1.7%) 远优于 post-hoc part pooling (+0.9%)
2. 纯 global feature，无需 part branch，架构极简
3. 额外参数仅 102K（两个 PSG 模块），几乎不增加计算量
4. 已超过 Phase 1 最佳

**下一步方向**:
  A. PSG + Part Pooling 组合 — 让 backbone 和 part branch 同时利用 pose
  B. PSG 消融实验 — 证明 PSG 每个组件的必要性
  C. PSG 在不同 stage 注入 — Stage 2 vs Stage 3 vs 全部 stages
  D. PSG 超参数分析 — hidden_dim, 是否 sigmoid, etc.

**选择**: A — PSG + Part Pooling 组合

**理由**:
1. PSG global feat (58.3%) 和 part-only feat (57.5%) 都有各自的优势
2. PSG 改善了 backbone 特征质量 → part features 也应该受益
3. 组合后可能进一步提升（PSG backbone + enhanced part features）
4. 如果组合有效，这就是完整的方法（backbone injection + part pooling = 全方位 pose 利用）

**执行结果**: exp008 mAP 57.7%, R1 66.0%。**组合不叠加**，低于 PSG-only (58.3%/67.9%)。Part pooling 的 part_only 测试模式丢弃了 PSG 增强的 global feature，而 part features 本身无法匹配 PSG-global 的质量。**结论：backbone-level injection 是更有效的 pose 利用方式，post-hoc pooling 在 PSG 基础上没有增量价值。**

### [2026-03-09 23:35] 决策 #9

**上下文**: exp007 (PSG) 和 exp008 (PSG+Part) 的对比揭示了重要规律：
1. PSG backbone injection: mAP 58.3% (+1.7%) — 全局特征，无 part branch
2. PSG + Part Pooling: mAP 57.7% (+1.1%) — part_only 测试，丢弃 global
3. Part Pooling alone: mAP 57.5% (+0.9%) — exp001

**核心洞察**:
- PSG 的增益主要来自改善全局特征质量，而 part pooling 依赖的是局部特征
- 两种方法的增益来源有重叠：都利用 pose heatmap 做 spatial attention
- 在 part_only 测试模式下，PSG 增强的 global 特征被浪费了

**选项**:
  A. PSG + concat 融合 — 保留 PSG global + part features，不丢弃全局特征
  B. 多 stage PSG — 在 Stage 2 也注入 PSG，更早引入 pose 先验
  C. PSG 改进 — 更强的 gate 机制（如 channel attention, multi-head gate）
  D. Backbone freeze warmup — 冻结 backbone 前 5 epochs，防止随机初始化模块破坏预训练

**选择**: 先做 B（多 stage PSG），这是架构级改进，有更大创新潜力

**理由**:
1. 当前 PSG 只在 Stage 3（2 个 block）注入，信息利用有限
2. 多 stage 注入可以让 pose 信息更早参与特征形成（Stage 2 的 24×8 分辨率对 pose heatmap 更有利）

# 实验结果总表 — Phase 2: Pure Pose Heatmap

## 数据集: Occluded-Duke

### 无后处理（纯模型结果）

| ID | 方法 | mAP | R-1 | R-5 | R-10 | vs Baseline | 备注 |

| 000 | Baseline (SOLIDER-Swin-Tiny, SW=0.2) | 56.6% | 66.5% | 79.4% | 83.4% | — | 120 epoch, 完美复现 |
| 001 | + Pose Part Pooling (sigmoid, 5 parts) | 57.1% | 66.7% | 78.4% | 83.0% | mAP+0.5%, R1+0.2% | Part 分类器收敛慢(id_part≈2.0 vs id_global≈0.2) |
| 001* | ↳ part-only 特征 | **57.5%** | **67.1%** | 79.1% | 83.5% | mAP+0.9%, R1+0.6% | Part 单独使用反而最好 |
| 002 | + Pose Part Pooling (spatial_softmax, T=1.0) | 57.2% | 66.4% | 79.2% | 83.5% | mAP+0.6%, R1-0.1% | 与 sigmoid 几乎相同 |
| 002* | ↳ part-only 特征 | **57.5%** | 66.8% | 79.6% | 84.0% | mAP+0.9%, R1+0.3% | 再次确认 part 单独使用更好 |
| 003 | Part-Dominant (67% part loss, part-only test) | 50.2% | 59.1% | 73.7% | 78.7% | mAP-6.4%, R1-7.4% | ❌ 降低 global weight 伤 backbone, ep60 终止 |
| 004 | + PFM (pose feature modulation, part-only test) | 57.5% | 66.3% | 79.6% | 84.1% | mAP+0.9%, R1-0.2% | 🟡 mAP 同 001*, R1 差 0.8%. PFM 加速收敛但不改善最终结果 |
| 004-g | ↳ global 特征 | 57.4% | 66.1% | 79.6% | 83.9% | mAP+0.8%, R1-0.4% | Global 略好于 001-global (57.1%), PFM 帮助 global |
| 005 | Stage 2 Part Pooling (24×8, 384ch, part-only) | 37.0%* | 44.8%* | 59.1%* | 65.2%* | mAP-19.6% | ❌ ep40 数据, ep49 OOM 终止. Stage 2 语义不足 |
| 006 | L2-norm concat (exp001 model, test-only) | 57.4% | 66.9% | 78.9% | 83.5% | mAP+0.8% | 🟡 比 concat(57.2%) 好, 但不如 part-only(57.5%) |
| **007** | **Pose Spatial Gate in Backbone (PSG)** | **58.3%** | **67.9%** | **80.8%** | **84.9%** | **mAP+1.7%, R1+1.4%** | **✅ 3-seed mean = 57.83% / 67.13%，所有 seed 均优于 baseline，PSG 有效** |
| 008 | PSG + Part Pooling (part_only test) | 57.7% | 66.0% | 78.3% | 82.8% | mAP+1.1%, R1-0.5% | 🟡 组合不叠加, 低于 PSG-only. Part pooling 拖累全局特征 |
| 009 | Multi-stage PSG (Stage 2+3) | 58.3% | 67.2% | 81.2% | 85.2% | mAP+1.7%, R1+0.7% | 🟡 mAP 匹配 exp007, R1 略低(-0.7%), R5/R10 略优. 多 156K params 无显著收益 |
| 010 | PSG + Backbone Freeze 5ep | 12.5%* | 17.5%* | 30.4%* | 36.7%* | — | ❌ ep30 终止. 冻结 backbone 导致灾难性特征损坏 |
| 011 | PSG Stage 3 (200 epochs) | 58.3% | 67.6% | 81.1% | 85.3% | mAP+1.7%, R1+1.1% | 🟡 与 exp007(120ep) mAP 相同, 75% 更多训练时间无收益 |
| 012 | Pose Attention Bias (PAB, Stage 3) | 57.4% | 67.3% | 81.4% | 86.2% | mAP+0.8%, R1+0.8% | 🟡 有效但弱于 PSG. 仅 5.4K params. 证明 feature gate > attn bias |
| 013 | PSG + PAB Combo (Stage 3) | 57.6% | 67.2% | 81.3% | 84.4% | mAP+1.0%, R1+0.7% | ❌ 双重注入互相干扰, 不如 PSG-only(-0.7% mAP). PAB 拖累 PSG 收敛 |
| 014 | PSG + Part Supervision (global test) | 57.6% | 65.8% | 77.9% | 82.6% | mAP+1.0%, R1-0.7% | ❌ 用 exp008 checkpoint 直接验证。Part supervision 梯度损害 PSG global feature |
| 015 | PSG Spatial (3×3 DWConv) | 58.3% | 67.1% | 81.4% | 85.8% | mAP+1.7%, R1+0.6% | 🟡 mAP 匹配 exp007, R1 低 0.8%. 3×3 conv 冗余，1×1 已足够 |

# 论文故事线（持续更新）

> **⚠️ Phase 1-4 内容保留在下方。Phase 5 更新如下。**

## PRCV Reset (2026-04-15) — PSG 主线 + GCN 结构补充

### 当前一句话故事

现有 pose-guided occluded ReID 大多在特征形成之后再使用 pose 信息；我们提出 `PSG`，将 pose 先验前移到 backbone 表征学习阶段，并在最终系统中引入 `GCN` 结构分支做显式 skeleton relational reasoning，形成 semantic-structural complementary evidence。

### 当前重审结论

这轮重审后，PRCV 主故事优先回到 `PSG`，而不是继续把 `LGPA-D + MaxSim + flip` 当主创新。

当前更稳的写法是：
1. **PSG** 是主创新点
2. **2-stage PSG** 只作为 `PSG` 的最终 instantiation / final configuration，不单独抢主贡献位置
3. **GCN 必须明确写进方法**，但定位为 *structural pose branch*，不是与 `PSG` 并列的第二主创新
4. `LGPA-D / OA-SD / PLBOA` 作为完整系统资产
5. `MaxSim / POT / flip` 只作为 supporting evaluation，不当训练端主贡献

### 当前主判断

1. `exp007` 已经足够支撑 `PSG` 本体：
   - 单次 `58.3 / 67.9`
   - 3-seed mean `57.83 / 67.13`
   - backbone-level pose injection 明确优于 post-hoc part pooling

2. `GCN` 应该被强调，但应强调其**作用位置**而不是单独吹成主创新：
   - `GCN` 的价值是提供显式 skeleton structure evidence
   - 更适合作为 `PSG` 支撑下的结构分支，而不是与 `PSG` 平行的主贡献
   - `exp249` 与 `exp246` 已经说明 `LGPA-D + GCN` 双分支具备稳定互补性

3. `2-stage PSG` 可以作为最终版本，但**不必在主叙事里和 1-stage 正面对打**
   - `exp009`、`exp251`、`exp253` 都说明：multi-stage 不会在所有 scaffold 上自动更强
   - 但 `exp255 vs exp255b` 明确说明：在 `GCN512` 这类高容量结构分支上，`2-stage PSG` 是关键条件

4. 因为实验都可以重跑，接下来不把旧消融当最终版，而是重新设计干净的 `PSG` stage 消融矩阵

### 当前最强系统 scaffold

当前训练端最强实验是 `exp255`：
- `Swin-Small + 2-stage PSG + LGPA-D + GCN512 + OA-SD + PLBOA`
- `FINAL = 73.2 / 83.3`

当前最关键的结构证据是：
- `exp255`: `GCN512 + 2-stage PSG = 73.2 / 83.3`
- `exp255b`: `GCN512 + 1-stage PSG = 71.5 / 81.9`

这组对照最适合在**消融**里写成：
> 最终实现采用 `2-stage PSG`；进一步对照显示，在高容量结构分支上，它比 `1-stage` 更能稳定支撑结构证据的发挥。

### 推荐写作口径

1. **标题 / 摘要 / 引言**
   - 只讲 `PSG`
   - 可以写：我们在 backbone 中间 stage 之间注入 pose 信息
   - 最多补一句：最终实现采用 two-stage instantiation

2. **方法部分**
   - 把 `PSG` 定义成一个通用的 pose-guided spatial gating 机制
   - 再说明：实际实验中采用 `2-stage PSG` 作为最终配置

3. **消融部分**
   - 再回答为什么最终选 `2-stage`
   - 用 `1-stage / 2-stage / 3-stage` 小表说明选择依据即可

### 论文里哪些模块要重点提及

1. **第一层：主贡献**
   - `PSG`
   - 写法：backbone 内的 pose-guided spatial gating

2. **第二层：关键支撑机制**
   - `GCN`
   - 写法：`GCN` 是 explicit structural pose reasoning branch
   - `2-stage PSG` 放在最终实现与消融选择中说明，不单列为主贡献

3. **第三层：完整系统资产**
   - `LGPA-D`
   - `OA-SD`
   - `PLBOA`
   - 写法：semantic branch + training recipe，不抢主创新位

4. **第四层：附加评测资产**
   - `MaxSim / POT / flip`
   - 写法：test-time supporting evaluations

### 推荐贡献点写法

1. 提出 `PSG`，在 backbone 内进行 pose-guided spatial gating，而不是在特征形成后再做 pose-aware pooling 或 filtering
2. 构建 semantic-structural complementary occluded ReID system，其中 `GCN` 提供显式 skeleton relational evidence，`LGPA-D` 提供语义 part evidence，与 `PSG` 形成互补
3. 在 Occluded-Duke 上系统验证该框架，并采用 `2-stage PSG` 作为最终实现；实验表明该设计能够更稳定地支撑高容量结构分支，最终在 Swin-Small 上得到当前最佳训练端结果之一

### 推荐摘要骨架

可按下面 4 句展开：

1. **问题句**
   - 现有 pose-guided occluded ReID 往往在特征提取完成后才利用 pose，因而对表征学习阶段的结构先验注入不足。

2. **方法句**
   - 我们提出 `PSG`，在 backbone 中间层通过 pose-conditioned spatial gating 直接调制特征形成过程。

3. **扩展句**
   - 在此基础上，我们结合 `GCN` 结构分支，以显式建模 skeleton relational evidence，并在最终实现中采用 `2-stage PSG` 作为具体配置，从而形成 semantic-structural complementary representation。

4. **结果句**
   - 在 Occluded-Duke 等基准上，该框架取得了当前项目最优结果之一，其中 `Swin-Small` 配置达到 `73.2 / 83.3`；消融进一步表明，最终采用的 `2-stage PSG` 更适合支撑高容量结构分支。

### 执行优先级

1. 重新设计 `PSG` 的干净 stage 消融：
   - no PSG
   - 1-stage PSG
   - 2-stage PSG
   - 3-stage PSG
2. 固定 branch 容量，单独验证 `2-stage PSG` 是否是高容量 `GCN` branch 的必要条件
3. 在此基础上，再决定最终论文标题更偏 `PSG` 还是 `Hierarchical PSG`

### 说明

详细重审与文献压缩总结见：
`experiments/paper_notes/2026-04-15_prcv_reset.md`

## Phase 5 Story Update (2026-04-08) — LGPA-D 时代

### 暂定标题
Language-Grounded Part Assembly for Occluded Person Re-Identification

### 当前最佳结果

| Backbone | Method | mAP (eq) | R1 (eq) | mAP (MaxSim) | R1 (MaxSim) |
|----------|--------|------|------|------|------|
| Tiny | LGPA-D+OA-SD | 65.3% | 75.7% | 66.0% | 76.4% |
| **Tiny** | **LGPA-D+GCN+OA-SD** | **65.5%** | **77.2%** | **66.3%** | **77.7%** |
| Small | LGPA-D+OA-SD (local) | 70.2% | 80.1% | 71.9% | 82.2% |
| **Small** | **LGPA-D+OA-SD (remote)** | **71.6%** | **81.6%** | **73.0%** | **82.7%** |
| Small | GCN+PAA+OA-SD (old baseline) | 70.6% | 82.6% | 72.3% | 82.9% |
| *Small* | *LGPA-D+GCN+OA-SD (exp249, 进行中)* | *TBD* | *TBD* | *TBD* | *TBD* |

### 核心贡献

1. **LGPA-D (Language-Grounded Part Assignment, Detached)**
   - 首次将 VLM (CLIP) 语义知识用于 occluded ReID 的 part assignment
   - 5 个语义 body parts: head, torso, arms, upper_legs, lower_legs
   - CLIP frozen text prototypes + cross-attention + pose heatmap bias
   - Detached from backbone → 不干扰训练, 全程 delta 为正
   - vs GCN skeleton features: +2.1% mAP (语义 > 结构)
   - vs PPA (non-detached): +4.4% (detach 消除后期干扰)

2. **PSG (Pose Spatial Gate)**
   - Backbone 内部 pose 注入 (Stage 3 block 间)
   - 轻量 102K params, +1.7% mAP
   - 改变特征形成方式, 不只是 post-hoc pooling

3. **Dual-Branch Architecture (LGPA-D + GCN)**
   - 语义 part features (LGPA-D) + 骨架 keypoint features (GCN) 正交互补
   - Tiny: +0.2 mAP / +1.5 R1 vs LGPA-D only
   - 两个 branch 都在 detached features 上操作

4. **MaxSim Hybrid Matching**
   - ColBERT-style late interaction 首次引入 person ReID
   - +1.0~1.5% mAP across all checkpoints
   - 理论框架: partial-set-to-partial-set matching

### 关键消融发现

1. **Detach barrier 是根本性约束**: 
   - Non-detached (exp243): ep80 -1.1 mAP → 后期干扰
   - Detached (exp244): ep120 +2.1 mAP → 全程正向
   - 250 实验验证: backbone 必须完全由主 loss 驱动

2. **CLIP 语义 > GCN 结构**:
   - LGPA-D 无 OA-SD (63.6) ≈ GCN + OA-SD (63.2)
   - CLIP 的 part assignment 能力 ≈ OA-SD 的训练增强

3. **训练集 95.8% visible**: 
   - 所有 visibility-dependent 训练方法失败 (VCSR, routing)
   - PLBOA (pixel-level occlusion augmentation) 是唯一有效补充

### 论文叙事

> Occluded person ReID 的核心挑战不是"如何处理遮挡"而是"如何定义和匹配不完整的身份证据"。
> 我们提出 LGPA (Language-Grounded Part Assembly): 利用 CLIP 的语义理解能力，
> 将 backbone 空间特征分解为语义 body parts，在 detached 特征上安全操作。
> 配合 PSG (backbone 内 pose 注入) 和 MaxSim (part-level late interaction matching)，
> 形成完整的 "语义引导提取 → 部分集合匹配" 框架。

---

## Phase 4 Story Update (2026-04-02)

### 当前最佳结果 (Phase 4 时期)

| Backbone | Method | mAP (eq) | R1 (eq) | mAP (maxsim) | R1 (maxsim) |
|----------|--------|------|------|------|------|
| Tiny | GCN+PAA+OA-SD | 63.2% | 75.4% | 64.2% | 77.1% |
| Tiny | **GCN+PAA+OA-SD+GSPB** | 62.9% | 74.3% | **64.6%** | **76.0%** |
| Small | GCN+PAA+OA-SD | 70.6% | 82.6% | 72.3% | 82.9% |
| Small | GCN+PAA+OA-SD+PKC | 70.6% | 81.8% | **72.4%** | **83.1%** |

### Phase 4 发现

1. **MaxSim Behavior on Tiny**: `MaxSim` 的收益更依赖 per-keypoint consistency，而不是简单取决于 global 强弱。

2. **GSPB (Gradient-Scaled Part Branch)**: 5% Part→Backbone 梯度大幅加速早期收敛 (+5.8% at ep10!) 但不改善 final。首次发现 detach 与 non-detach 之间的中间解。

3. **OA-SD Teacher Fix**: 修复了 EMA teacher 的 Dropout/DropPath/BN 噪声问题。修复后 teacher 更稳定，但 final 结果不变（EMA 的自修正性）。

4. **per-keypoint training loss 全面证伪**: PKC, MST, PACI, OERL, BA-PKC — 10 个实验全部失败。根本原因: detached GCN 阻断梯度到 backbone，non-detached 与 CE 冲突。

---

## Phase 3 Story Update (2026-03-23)

### 暂定标题
Pose-Guided Structural Token Decomposition for Occluded Person Re-Identification

### 核心贡献（更新 2026-03-24）

 succeeded in 0ms:
     1	# encoding: utf-8
     2	"""
     3	CVPB training loop on CARGO = the afd_reid baseline trainer + OVP-Mem module.
     4	
     5	This reuses the dataset / model / eval from ../afd_reid unchanged.  The only
     6	addition vs afd_reid/afd_train.py is the **OVP-Mem (Opposite-View Prototype
     7	Memory)** option, gated by --ovp (default OFF -> reproduces the BoT baseline).
     8	
     9	Baseline (no flags):
    10	    resnet50(IMAGENET1K_V1) + GeM + BNNeck
    11	    loss = CE(label-smooth 0.1) + batch-hard triplet (margin 0.3)
    12	    PK sampler P=16 x K=4 (bs=64), AdamW lr 3.5e-4, 10-ep warmup + cosine, 60 ep.
    13	    eval every 10 ep: A->G and G->A cross-view mAP / R1 / mINP.
    14	
    15	--ovp (OVP-Mem):
    16	    Maintain, per train pid, two EMA prototypes (aerial / ground) of the
    17	    L2-normalized BNNeck feature in a register_buffer of shape
    18	    [num_pid, 2, feat_dim]; EMA momentum 0.2.  Each step:
    19	      1) update the prototypes of the pids/views present in the batch (EMA),
    20	      2) add an InfoNCE loss pulling each sample toward its OWN pid's
    21	         OPPOSITE-view prototype and away from all other pids' opposite-view
    22	         prototypes:  CE( cos(z, P[:, opp_view]) / tau ,  y ).
    23	    total = CE + triplet + lambda_ovp * OVP.   (batch size unchanged, bs=64.)
    24	
    25	    Cold-start handling: a sample only contributes to the OVP loss once its own
    26	    opposite-view prototype has been initialized (seen >=1 time); candidate
    27	    columns that are still uninitialized are masked out of the InfoNCE logits so
    28	    a zero prototype can never act as an easy negative/positive.
    29	
    30	Run on lab-3090:
    31	    cd /root/work/SOLIDER-REID/experiments/cargo_cvpb
    32	    PYTHONUNBUFFERED=1 python afd_train.py \
    33	        --data_root /root/work/SOLIDER-REID/data \
    34	        --out_dir   /root/work/SOLIDER-REID/log/cargo/cvpb_ovp \
    35	        --ovp \
    36	        2>&1 | tee /tmp/cvpb_ovp.log
    37	    # baseline reproduction: drop --ovp
    38	"""
    39	import os
    40	import sys
    41	import time
    42	import math
    43	import random
    44	import argparse
    45	
    46	import numpy as np
    47	import torch
    48	import torch.nn as nn
    49	import torch.nn.functional as F
    50	from torch.utils.data import DataLoader
    51	
    52	# reuse afd_reid building blocks unchanged
    53	sys.path.insert(0, os.path.join(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)),
    54	                                '..', 'afd_reid'))
    55	from cargo_dataset import (CARGO, CARGOImageDataset, build_transforms,  # noqa: E402
    56	                           RandomIdentitySampler, filter_by_view)
    57	from afd_model import build_model  # noqa: E402
    58	# reuse the exact loss / schedule / eval helpers so nothing diverges
    59	from afd_train import (CrossEntropyLabelSmooth, TripletLoss, WarmupCosineLR,  # noqa: E402
    60	                       run_cross_view_eval, print_eval, set_seed)
    61	
    62	
    63	# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
    64	# OVP-Mem: opposite-view prototype memory
    65	# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
    66	class OVPMemory(nn.Module):
    67	    """Per-pid, per-view EMA prototype bank + opposite-view InfoNCE loss.
    68	
    69	    bank: register_buffer [num_pid, 2, feat_dim]  (view 0 = Aerial, 1 = Ground)
    70	    init: register_buffer [num_pid, 2] uint8 = has this (pid,view) been seen.
    71	
    72	    Prototypes are L2-normalized; features used for update/loss are the
    73	    L2-normalized BNNeck features (detached for the EMA update).
    74	    """
    75	
    76	    def __init__(self, num_pid, feat_dim, momentum=0.2, tau=0.05):
    77	        super().__init__()
    78	        self.num_pid = num_pid
    79	        self.feat_dim = feat_dim
    80	        self.momentum = momentum          # weight on the *new* batch mean
    81	        self.tau = tau
    82	        self.register_buffer('bank', torch.zeros(num_pid, 2, feat_dim))
    83	        self.register_buffer('inited', torch.zeros(num_pid, 2, dtype=torch.uint8))
    84	
    85	    @torch.no_grad()
    86	    def update(self, feats, labels, views):
    87	        """EMA-update prototypes for the (pid,view) groups present in the batch.
    88	
    89	        feats:(B,D) L2-normed BN feats (detached). labels:(B,) long.
    90	        views:(B,) long in {0,1}.  Per group, EMA toward the group mean, then
    91	        re-normalize the prototype.
    92	        """
    93	        feats = feats.detach()
    94	        for v in (0, 1):
    95	            vmask = (views == v)
    96	            if not torch.any(vmask):
    97	                continue
    98	            fv = feats[vmask]
    99	            lv = labels[vmask]
   100	            uniq = torch.unique(lv)
   101	            for pid in uniq:
   102	                pid_i = int(pid)
   103	                gmean = fv[lv == pid].mean(dim=0)
   104	                gmean = F.normalize(gmean, dim=0)
   105	                if self.inited[pid_i, v] == 0:
   106	                    self.bank[pid_i, v] = gmean
   107	                    self.inited[pid_i, v] = 1
   108	                else:
   109	                    self.bank[pid_i, v] = ((1 - self.momentum) * self.bank[pid_i, v]
   110	                                           + self.momentum * gmean)
   111	                    self.bank[pid_i, v] = F.normalize(self.bank[pid_i, v], dim=0)
   112	
   113	    def loss(self, feats, labels, views):
   114	        """InfoNCE: each sample classified against ALL pids' opposite-view
   115	        prototypes, target = its own pid.  feats:(B,D) L2-normed BN feats
   116	        (NOT detached -> gradient flows to the encoder).
   117	
   118	        Returns a scalar loss (0 if no sample has an initialized own-opp proto).
   119	        """
   120	        device = feats.device
   121	        opp = 1 - views                                   # opposite view per sample
   122	        total = feats.new_zeros(())
   123	        count = 0
   124	        for v_opp in (0, 1):
   125	            # samples whose opposite view == v_opp (i.e. samples from view 1-v_opp)
   126	            smask = (opp == v_opp)
   127	            if not torch.any(smask):
   128	                continue
   129	            protos = self.bank[:, v_opp, :]               # (num_pid, D)
   130	            valid = (self.inited[:, v_opp] == 1)          # (num_pid,) candidate cols
   131	            if valid.sum() == 0:
   132	                continue
   133	            z = feats[smask]                              # (b,D)
   134	            y = labels[smask]                             # (b,)
   135	            # only keep samples whose OWN opposite-view proto exists
   136	            own_ok = valid[y]
   137	            if own_ok.sum() == 0:
   138	                continue
   139	            z = z[own_ok]
   140	            y = y[own_ok]
   141	            logits = (z @ protos.t()) / self.tau          # (b, num_pid)
   142	            # mask out uninitialized candidate columns
   143	            logits = logits.masked_fill(~valid.view(1, -1), float('-inf'))
   144	            total = total + F.cross_entropy(logits, y)
   145	            count += 1
   146	        if count == 0:
   147	            return feats.new_zeros(())
   148	        return total / count
   149	
   150	
   151	# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
   152	# train
   153	# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
   154	def main():
   155	    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()
   156	    ap.add_argument('--data_root', default='/root/work/SOLIDER-REID/data')
   157	    ap.add_argument('--out_dir', default='./log/cargo/cvpb_ovp')
   158	    ap.add_argument('--img_size', type=int, nargs=2, default=[256, 128])
   159	    ap.add_argument('--epochs', type=int, default=60)
   160	    ap.add_argument('--warmup_epochs', type=int, default=10)
   161	    ap.add_argument('--lr', type=float, default=3.5e-4)
   162	    ap.add_argument('--weight_decay', type=float, default=5e-4)
   163	    ap.add_argument('--P', type=int, default=16)
   164	    ap.add_argument('--K', type=int, default=4)
   165	    ap.add_argument('--test_batch', type=int, default=128)
   166	    ap.add_argument('--workers', type=int, default=8)
   167	    ap.add_argument('--margin', type=float, default=0.3)
   168	    ap.add_argument('--label_smooth', type=float, default=0.1)
   169	    ap.add_argument('--eval_period', type=int, default=10)
   170	    ap.add_argument('--seed', type=int, default=1)
   171	    ap.add_argument('--no_amp', action='store_true', help='disable mixed precision')
   172	    # model switches (keep AFD off by default -> pure BoT baseline + OVP)
   173	    ap.add_argument('--last_stride', type=int, default=1)
   174	    ap.add_argument('--pool', default='gem', choices=['gem', 'avg'])
   175	    ap.add_argument('--use_afd', action='store_true')
   176	    ap.add_argument('--afd_router', type=int, default=1)
   177	    ap.add_argument('--afd_cvfc', type=int, default=1)
   178	    ap.add_argument('--afd_stage', default='layer1',
   179	                    choices=['stem', 'layer1', 'layer2'])
   180	    ap.add_argument('--router_cond_view', type=int, default=1)
   181	    ap.add_argument('--low_r', type=float, default=0.125)
   182	    ap.add_argument('--mid_r', type=float, default=0.30)
   183	    ap.add_argument('--high_drop_p', type=float, default=0.5)
   184	    ap.add_argument('--w_cvfc', type=float, default=0.5)
   185	    # --- OVP-Mem ---
   186	    ap.add_argument('--ovp', action='store_true',
   187	                    help='enable OVP-Mem opposite-view prototype InfoNCE loss')
   188	    ap.add_argument('--ovp_lambda', type=float, default=0.5,
   189	                    help='weight of the OVP InfoNCE loss')
   190	    ap.add_argument('--ovp_tau', type=float, default=0.05,
   191	                    help='temperature for the OVP InfoNCE logits')
   192	    ap.add_argument('--ovp_momentum', type=float, default=0.2,
   193	                    help='EMA momentum = weight on the new batch mean')
   194	    ap.add_argument('--ovp_warmup', type=int, default=10,
   195	                    help='H1 fix: warmup OVP lambda linearly over this many epochs')
   196	    args = ap.parse_args()
   197	    args.afd_router = bool(args.afd_router)
   198	    args.afd_cvfc = bool(args.afd_cvfc)
   199	    args.router_cond_view = bool(args.router_cond_view)
   200	
   201	    set_seed(args.seed)
   202	    os.makedirs(args.out_dir, exist_ok=True)
   203	    device = 'cuda'
   204	    batch_size = args.P * args.K
   205	
   206	    print("=" * 70)
   207	    print("CVPB-ReID training (afd_reid baseline + OVP-Mem)")
   208	    print(f"  use_afd={args.use_afd}  ovp={args.ovp} "
   209	          f"(lambda={args.ovp_lambda} tau={args.ovp_tau} mom={args.ovp_momentum})")
   210	    print(f"  bs={batch_size} (P={args.P} K={args.K}) lr={args.lr} "
   211	          f"epochs={args.epochs} warmup={args.warmup_epochs} amp={not args.no_amp}")
   212	    print(f"  out_dir={args.out_dir}")
   213	    print("=" * 70)
   214	
   215	    # data
   216	    dataset = CARGO(root=args.data_root, verbose=True)
   217	    train_tf = build_transforms(is_train=True, img_size=tuple(args.img_size))
   218	    train_set = CARGOImageDataset(dataset.train, train_tf)
   219	    sampler = RandomIdentitySampler(dataset.train, batch_size, args.K)
   220	    train_loader = DataLoader(train_set, batch_size=batch_size, sampler=sampler,
   221	                              num_workers=args.workers, pin_memory=True,
   222	                              drop_last=True)
   223	
   224	    # model
   225	    model = build_model(dataset.num_train_pids, args).to(device)
   226	
   227	    # losses
   228	    ce = CrossEntropyLabelSmooth(dataset.num_train_pids, args.label_smooth)
   229	    tri = TripletLoss(args.margin)
   230	
   231	    # OVP memory (buffers live on the model device; not optimized)
   232	    ovp = None
   233	    if args.ovp:
   234	        ovp = OVPMemory(dataset.num_train_pids, model.in_planes,
   235	                        momentum=args.ovp_momentum, tau=args.ovp_tau).to(device)
   236	
   237	    # optimizer
   238	    optimizer = torch.optim.AdamW(model.parameters(), lr=args.lr,
   239	                                  weight_decay=args.weight_decay)
   240	    scheduler = WarmupCosineLR(optimizer, args.warmup_epochs, args.epochs)
   241	    scaler = torch.amp.GradScaler('cuda', enabled=not args.no_amp)
   242	
   243	    view_map = {'Aerial': 0, 'Ground': 1}
   244	    best_map = -1.0
   245	    best_epoch = -1
   246	    n_iter_total = len(train_loader)
   247	
   248	    for epoch in range(1, args.epochs + 1):
   249	        model.train()
   250	        t0 = time.time()
   251	        # H1 fix: warmup OVP lambda over ovp_warmup epochs to avoid cold-start gradient spikes
   252	        ovp_lambda_eff = (args.ovp_lambda * min(1.0, epoch / max(1, args.ovp_warmup))) if args.ovp else 0.0
   253	        meters = {'loss': 0.0, 'ce': 0.0, 'tri': 0.0, 'ovp': 0.0, 'acc': 0.0}
   254	        seen = 0
   255	
   256	        for it, batch in enumerate(train_loader):
   257	            imgs = batch['img'].to(device, non_blocking=True)
   258	            labels = batch['pid'].to(device, non_blocking=True)
   259	            views = torch.tensor([view_map[v] for v in batch['view']],
   260	                                 device=device)
   261	            vidx = views if args.use_afd else None
   262	
   263	            optimizer.zero_grad()
   264	            with torch.amp.autocast('cuda', enabled=not args.no_amp):
   265	                out = model(imgs, view_idx=vidx,
   266	                            return_cvfc=(args.use_afd and args.afd_cvfc))
   267	                logits = out['logits']
   268	                gfeat = out['global_feat']
   269	                bn = out['bn_feat']
   270	                loss_ce = ce(logits, labels)
   271	                loss_tri = tri(gfeat, labels)
   272	                loss = loss_ce + loss_tri
   273	
   274	                loss_ovp = torch.zeros((), device=device)
   275	                if args.ovp:
   276	                    # OVP loss in fp32 for numerical safety (cosine + softmax)
   277	                    z = F.normalize(bn.float(), dim=1)
   278	                    loss_ovp = ovp.loss(z, labels, views)
   279	                    loss = loss + ovp_lambda_eff * loss_ovp
   280	
   281	            scaler.scale(loss).backward()
   282	            scaler.step(optimizer)
   283	            scaler.update()
   284	
   285	            # update prototypes AFTER the optimizer step, with detached feats
   286	            if args.ovp:
   287	                with torch.no_grad():
   288	                    ovp.update(F.normalize(bn.detach().float(), dim=1),
   289	                               labels, views)
   290	
   291	            bs = imgs.size(0)
   292	            seen += bs
   293	            meters['loss'] += loss.item() * bs
   294	            meters['ce'] += loss_ce.item() * bs
   295	            meters['tri'] += loss_tri.item() * bs
   296	            meters['ovp'] += float(loss_ovp) * bs
   297	            meters['acc'] += (logits.argmax(1) == labels).float().sum().item()
   298	
   299	            if (it + 1) % 50 == 0 or (it + 1) == n_iter_total:
   300	                lr = optimizer.param_groups[0]['lr']
   301	                print(f"Epoch[{epoch}] Iter[{it + 1}/{n_iter_total}] "
   302	                      f"Loss: {meters['loss'] / seen:.3f} "
   303	                      f"CE: {meters['ce'] / seen:.3f} "
   304	                      f"Tri: {meters['tri'] / seen:.3f} "
   305	                      f"OVP: {meters['ovp'] / seen:.4f} "
   306	                      f"Acc: {meters['acc'] / seen:.3f} LR: {lr:.2e}")
   307	
   308	        scheduler.step()
   309	        dt = time.time() - t0
   310	        ovp_msg = ""
   311	        if args.ovp:
   312	            ovp_msg = (f" OVP[lam_eff={ovp_lambda_eff:.3f} "
   313	                       f"inited={int(ovp.inited.sum())}/{2 * ovp.num_pid}]")
   314	        print(f"Epoch[{epoch}] done in {dt:.1f}s  "
   315	              f"Loss={meters['loss'] / seen:.3f} Acc={meters['acc'] / seen:.3f}{ovp_msg}")
   316	
   317	        if epoch % args.eval_period == 0 or epoch == args.epochs:
   318	            results = run_cross_view_eval(model, dataset, args, device)
   319	            mean_map = print_eval(epoch, results)
   320	            if mean_map > best_map:
   321	                best_map = mean_map
   322	                best_epoch = epoch
   323	                torch.save(model.state_dict(),
   324	                           os.path.join(args.out_dir, 'model_best.pth'))
   325	                print(f"    * new best mean mAP={best_map:.2f} (epoch {epoch}) saved")
   326	
   327	    torch.save(model.state_dict(), os.path.join(args.out_dir, 'model_final.pth'))
   328	    print("=" * 70)
   329	    print(f"Training finished. Best mean A<->G mAP={best_map:.2f} @ epoch {best_epoch}")
   330	    print(f"Checkpoints in {args.out_dir}")
   331	    print("=" * 70)
   332	
   333	
   334	if __name__ == '__main__':
   335	    main()

# 创新点头脑风暴 — Phase 2: Pure Pose Heatmap

## 2026-04-15 PRCV 重审：回到 PSG 主线，重做 multi-stage 消融

### 这轮重审后的核心判断

1. `PSG` 仍然是当前最稳的主创新点  
   - `exp007` 单次 `58.3 / 67.9`
   - 3-seed mean `57.83 / 67.13`
   - backbone injection 明确优于 post-hoc pooling

2. `2-stage PSG` 有希望作为最终版本，但现有证据还不够干净  
   - `exp009 / exp251 / exp253` 说明 multi-stage **不是普遍自动更优**
   - `exp255 vs exp255b` 又强烈说明：在 `GCN512` 结构分支下，`2-stage PSG` 是关键条件

3. `LGPA-D + GCN + OA-SD + MaxSim + flip` 更适合写成 supporting system，而不是主创新本体  
   - `LGPA-D` 像 detached semantic part asset
   - `MaxSim/POT/flip` 主要是 test-time
   - `exp257-259` 已说明 recipe 空间基本耗尽

### 当前真正该补的不是新故事，而是干净消融

用户已明确说明所有实验都可以重跑，因此下一步最该做的是：

1. 不再把旧结果当最终消融闭环
2. 重新设计 `PSG` / `2-stage PSG` / `3-stage PSG` 的干净对照
3. 把“multi-stage PSG 什么时候有用”这件事说清楚

### 当前推荐验证顺序

1. **基础 PSG 消融**
   - no PSG
   - 1-stage PSG
   - 2-stage PSG
   - 3-stage PSG

2. **结构分支依赖性消融**
   - GCN256 + 1-stage
   - GCN256 + 2-stage
   - GCN512 + 1-stage
   - GCN512 + 2-stage

3. **必要时再补 semantic 分支依赖性**
   - LGPA-only + 1-stage / 2-stage
   - LGPA+GCN + 1-stage / 2-stage

### 当前主线口径

从现在开始，PRCV 方向优先写成：

- `PSG` = 主创新
- `2-stage PSG` = scalable extension / 当前最终版本
- `LGPA-D / GCN / OA-SD / MaxSim` = supporting assets

### 参考

详细文献压缩与路线说明见：
`experiments/paper_notes/2026-04-15_prcv_reset.md`

## 前轮教训 (Phase 1, 33 experiments)
- ViTPose visibility 向量不够可靠（AP 相关性仅 0.237）
- 中间层 visibility modulation 有害（破坏预训练空间结构），但这是 visibility 特有问题
- PCFC alpha suppression 是该框架特有的脆弱平衡点
- NFC test-time 方法有效但不算训练端创新
- **关键结论**: 不要用 visibility 向量，用原始 pose 热图

---

## Phase 2 实验总结 (exp001-exp021, 21 个实验)

### 已证实的核心发现

**1. Backbone Injection > Post-hoc Pooling**
- PSG (特征形成阶段注入 pose) +1.7% mAP，仅 102K params
- Part Pooling (特征形成后用 pose 选择) +0.9% mAP，~2.6M params
- PFM (后置调制) 中性效果
- **结论**: 让 backbone 在特征提取过程中知道人体结构，比事后选择更有效

**2. PSG 对空间级干扰敏感，但通道级正交不干扰**
- PSG + PAB Combo: ❌ (-0.7% vs PSG-only)
- PSG + Part Pooling: ❌ (-0.6% vs PSG-only)
- PSG + Part Supervision (global test): ❌ (-0.7% mAP, -2.1% R1)
- PSG + PCG (通道级 gate): 🟡 持平 (mAP 58.0%, -0.3% vs PSG-only)
- **核心瓶颈**: 在同一个 Stage 3 上叠加任何模块都会梯度干扰

**3. 复杂度越高，效果越差**
- 有效方法排序: PSG(58.3%) > PCG-only(57.8%) > PRA(57.8%) > Part Pooling(57.5%) > PAB(57.4%) > PXA(57.3%) > CAPSG(57.2%)
- PXA/CAPSG 最复杂，效果最差
- **PSG 的极简性就是它的优势**

**4. PSG 跨数据集/backbone 均有效（4090 验证）**

| 数据集 | Backbone | PSG mAP 提升 |
|--------|----------|-------------|
| Occluded-Duke | Swin-Tiny | +1.7% |
| Occluded-Duke | Swin-Small (lr4) | +2.0% |
| Market-1501 | Swin-Tiny | +0.8% |
| Market-1501 | Swin-Small (lr4) | +0.6% |

### 关键教训
- **梯度干扰是核心瓶颈**: 21 个实验中所有在 PSG 基础上"加东西"的尝试都失败了，原因是同一个 Stage 3 内的模块共享梯度流
- **要突破 PSG，必须用独立的处理路径**，避免梯度干扰
- **可以添加大模块**: 用户确认不限于轻量模块，可以加 ResNet 分支、Decoder、GCN 等

---

## Phase 3: 新方向候选 (2025.03.11 Web 搜索 + 文献调研)

### ★ 方向 K: 双分支架构 — Pose-Guided Dual-Stream (PDS)
**优先级: ⭐⭐⭐⭐⭐**

```
Input → Swin Stage 1-2 (共享)
              ↓
    ┌─────────┴─────────┐
    ↓                   ↓
  Stage 3-A           Stage 3-B (独立权重)
  + PSG               + Pose Part Processing
    ↓                   ↓
   GAP              Part Pooling (基于 heatmap)
    ↓                   ↓
 Global Feat        Part Feats
    ↓                   ↓
 ID + Triplet      Part ID + Part Triplet
    ↓                   ↓
    └── concat (test) ──┘
```

- **核心想法**: 解决 exp008/014 暴露的梯度干扰问题。复制独立的 Stage 3 给 Part 分支。
- **为什么与前轮不同**: exp008 在同一 Stage 3 上做 PSG+Part，梯度干扰。PDS 用独立 Stage 3，各自优化。
- **代价**: ~6M 额外 params（Stage 3 ≈ 2 SwinBlocks × 768ch）
- **优势**: Part 分支可以集成更多 pose 操作（GCN、cross-attention），而不影响 Global 分支
- **参考**: PGFL-KD (ACM MM 2021) 三分支架构, FCFormer (TPAMI 2024) 双流设计
- **论文定位**: 主贡献之一 — "dual-stream pose-guided architecture with gradient-isolated part learning"

### ★ 方向 L: 关键点相对位置编码 (KP-RPE)
**优先级: ⭐⭐⭐⭐**

- **来源**: CVPR 2024 人脸识别 (Kim et al., "KeyPoint Relative Position Encoding for Face Recognition")
- **核心想法**: 将 Swin 的 attention bias 从像素相对位置改为关键点相对位置
  - 标准 RPE: bias(i,j) = table[xi-xj, yi-yj]
  - KP-RPE: bias(i,j) = MLP(dist(i,kp1),...,dist(j,kp1),...)
- **与 PSG 完全正交**: PSG 调制 feature 幅度（乘法），KP-RPE 调制 attention 路由（加法 bias）
- **与 exp012 PAB 的区别**: PAB 是单像素 spatial map，KP-RPE 编码 token 对之间的**结构关系**
- **参数量**: ~5-10K，几乎零开销
- **风险**: Swin 用 window attention (7×7 window)，12×4 feature map 上 window 划分可能限制效果
- **论文定位**: 与 PSG 叠加使用 — "spatial gating + structural attention routing"

### ★ 方向 M: 骨架图卷积特征传播 (Skeleton GCN)
**优先级: ⭐⭐⭐⭐**

```
PSG-enhanced Stage 3 features (12×4, 768ch)
              ↓
Bilinear sample at 17 keypoint locations → (17, 768)
              ↓
Skeleton GCN (2-3 layers, COCO 19 bone edges)
              ↓
可见部位特征 沿骨骼边传播到遮挡部位
              ↓
Part Feat Pool → concat with Global
```

- **核心价值**: 遮挡补全 — 当下半身被遮挡时，GCN 沿"髋→膝→踝"传播上半身特征
- **参考**: Tran-GCN (IET 2025), skeleton action recognition 领域成熟技术
- **参数量**: ~3-4M (17 nodes × 768 features × 2-3 GCN layers)
- **与 PDS (方向 K) 的结合**: 可以作为 Part 分支的核心模块
- **风险**: 前 21 个实验显示 post-backbone 方法收益有限，但 GCN 提供了全新的结构推理能力
- **论文定位**: "skeleton-topology-aware feature propagation for occlusion recovery"

### 方向 N: ControlNet-Style 加法注入
**优先级: ⭐⭐⭐**

- **来源**: ControlNet (ICCV 2023), LLaMA-Adapter
- **核心想法**: 复制 Stage 3 为 "pose encoder branch"，处理 pose heatmaps，通过 zero-conv 加法注入主干
- **与 PSG 的区别**: PSG 是乘法 x*(1+gate)，ControlNet 是加法 x + zero_conv(pose_feat)
- **可以和 PDS 整合**: 就是 PDS 的 Part 分支不做独立 pooling，而是加法 inject 回 Global 分支
- **参数量**: ~6M（完整 Stage 3 clone）或 ~100K（轻量 conv encoder）

### 方向 O: Pose Attention Supervision (PAS)
**优先级: ⭐⭐⭐**

- **来源**: PAFormer (arXiv 2024)
- **核心想法**: 不把 pose 热图作为输入，而是作为 attention 的**监督信号**。训练时让 attention map 匹配 pose heatmap 分布，推理时不需要 pose。
- **优势**: 零推理开销，改变 backbone 的内在表征
- **风险**: Swin window attention 使监督复杂化

### 方向 P: Pose-Guided Token Pruning
**优先级: ⭐⭐⭐**

- **来源**: PrATo (2025), HeatViT, Zero-TPrune (CVPR 2024)
- **核心想法**: 用 pose 热图计算 token 重要性，剪掉背景/遮挡物 token，只保留人体区域
- **效果**: 不仅加速推理，还从根本上消除遮挡物对特征的污染
- **与 PSG 的区别**: PSG 给所有 token 不同权重，token pruning 直接删除无关 token
- **挑战**: Swin window attention 对 token 数量有要求

### 方向 Q: 特征补全 (Feature Completion)
**优先级: ⭐⭐⭐**

- **来源**: FCFormer (TPAMI 2024)
- **核心想法**: 用 pose 热图识别遮挡区域（热图响应低），用 learnable tokens + decoder 重建遮挡区域特征
- **与 Skeleton GCN 的区别**: GCN 沿骨架边传播，Feature Completion 用 decoder 直接预测
- **可以结合 PDS**: 作为 Part 分支的特征补全模块

---

## 推荐实验路线

### Round 1: 大架构实验
1. **exp022: PDS (双分支)** — 最有潜力，解决核心梯度干扰问题
2. **exp023: KP-RPE** — 最轻量，与 PSG 正交，快速验证

### Round 2: 基于 Round 1 结果深化
- 如果 PDS 有效 → 在 Part 分支内集成 Skeleton GCN (方向 M)
- 如果 KP-RPE 有效 → PSG + KP-RPE 组合
- 如果都有效 → PDS + KP-RPE 组合

### Round 3: 论文补充实验
- 消融实验（PDS 各组件贡献）
- 效率分析（参数量、FLOPs、推理速度）

     2	"""
     3	AFD-ReID training loop on CARGO.
     4	
     5	Baseline (default, --use_afd off):
     6	    resnet50(IMAGENET1K_V1) + GeM + BNNeck
     7	    loss = CE(label smoothing 0.1) + batch-hard triplet (margin 0.3)
     8	    PK sampler P=16 x K=4 (bs=64), AdamW lr 3.5e-4, 10-ep warmup + cosine, 60 epochs.
     9	    eval every 10 epochs: A->G and G->A cross-view mAP / Rank-1 / mINP.
    10	
    11	AFD (--use_afd) adds the Frequency Reliability Router + Cross-View Frequency
    12	Counterfactual losses on top; everything else identical for a clean ablation.
    13	
    14	Run on lab-3090:
    15	    cd /root/work/SOLIDER-REID/experiments/afd_reid
    16	    PYTHONUNBUFFERED=1 python afd_train.py \
    17	        --data_root /root/work/SOLIDER-REID/data \
    18	        --out_dir   /root/work/SOLIDER-REID/log/cargo/afd_baseline \

    20	    # AFD variant: add  --use_afd

    42	# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
    43	# losses
    44	# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
    45	class CrossEntropyLabelSmooth(nn.Module):
    46	    def __init__(self, num_classes, epsilon=0.1):
    47	        super().__init__()
    48	        self.num_classes = num_classes
    49	        self.epsilon = epsilon
    50	        self.logsoftmax = nn.LogSoftmax(dim=1)
    51	
    52	    def forward(self, inputs, targets):
    53	        log_probs = self.logsoftmax(inputs)
    54	        targets = torch.zeros_like(log_probs).scatter_(
    55	            1, targets.unsqueeze(1), 1)
    56	        targets = (1 - self.epsilon) * targets + self.epsilon / self.num_classes
    57	        return (-targets * log_probs).mean(0).sum()
    58	
    59	
    60	def euclidean_dist(x, y):
    61	    m, n = x.size(0), y.size(0)
    62	    xx = torch.pow(x, 2).sum(1, keepdim=True).expand(m, n)
    63	    yy = torch.pow(y, 2).sum(1, keepdim=True).expand(n, m).t()
    64	    dist = xx + yy
    65	    dist = dist - 2 * (x @ y.t())
    66	    return dist.clamp(min=1e-12).sqrt()
    67	
    68	
    69	class TripletLoss(nn.Module):
    70	    """Batch-hard triplet (Hermans et al.) with margin."""
    71	
    72	    def __init__(self, margin=0.3):
    73	        super().__init__()
    74	        self.margin = margin
    75	        self.ranking_loss = nn.MarginRankingLoss(margin=margin)
    76	
    77	    def forward(self, feats, labels):
    78	        n = feats.size(0)
    79	        dist = euclidean_dist(feats, feats)
    80	        mask = labels.expand(n, n).eq(labels.expand(n, n).t())
    81	        dist_ap, dist_an = [], []
    82	        for i in range(n):
    83	            pos = dist[i][mask[i]]
    84	            neg = dist[i][mask[i] == 0]
    85	            dist_ap.append(pos.max().unsqueeze(0))
    86	            dist_an.append(neg.min().unsqueeze(0))
    87	        dist_ap = torch.cat(dist_ap)
    88	        dist_an = torch.cat(dist_an)
    89	        y = torch.ones_like(dist_an)
    90	        return self.ranking_loss(dist_an, dist_ap, y)
    91	
    92	
    93	# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
    94	# LR schedule: linear warmup -> cosine
    95	# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
    96	class WarmupCosineLR(torch.optim.lr_scheduler._LRScheduler):
    97	    def __init__(self, optimizer, warmup_epochs, max_epochs, warmup_factor=0.01,
    98	                 last_epoch=-1):
    99	        self.warmup_epochs = warmup_epochs
   100	        self.max_epochs = max_epochs
   101	        self.warmup_factor = warmup_factor
   102	        super().__init__(optimizer, last_epoch)
   103	
   104	    def get_lr(self):
   105	        e = self.last_epoch
   106	        if e < self.warmup_epochs:
   107	            alpha = e / max(1, self.warmup_epochs)
   108	            factor = self.warmup_factor * (1 - alpha) + alpha
   109	        else:
   110	            prog = (e - self.warmup_epochs) / max(1, self.max_epochs - self.warmup_epochs)
   111	            factor = 0.5 * (1 + math.cos(math.pi * prog))
   112	        return [base_lr * factor for base_lr in self.base_lrs]
   113	
   114	
   115	# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
   116	# evaluation: market-style mAP / CMC / mINP with same-pid&cam junk removal
   117	# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
   118	@torch.no_grad()
   119	def extract_features(model, loader, device, use_afd):
   120	    model.eval()
   121	    feats, pids, camids = [], [], []
   122	    view_map = {'Aerial': 0, 'Ground': 1}
   123	    for batch in loader:
   124	        imgs = batch['img'].to(device, non_blocking=True)
   125	        vidx = torch.tensor([view_map[v] for v in batch['view']],
   126	                            device=device) if use_afd else None
   127	        f = model(imgs, view_idx=vidx)
   128	        feats.append(f.cpu())
   129	        pids.append(batch['pid'])
   130	        camids.append(batch['camid'])
   131	    if not feats:   # empty split (degenerate direction)
   132	        return torch.empty(0), np.empty(0, dtype=np.int64), np.empty(0, dtype=np.int64)
   133	    feats = torch.cat(feats, 0)
   134	    pids = torch.cat(pids, 0).numpy()
   135	    camids = torch.cat(camids, 0).numpy()
   136	    return feats, pids, camids
   137	
   138	
   139	def eval_market(qf, q_pids, q_camids, gf, g_pids, g_camids, max_rank=50):
   140	    """Standard market1501 eval. Removes gallery items with same (pid,camid) as query.
   141	
   142	    Returns (mAP, cmc[max_rank], mINP).
   143	    """
   144	    if qf.numel() == 0 or gf.numel() == 0:
   145	        # degenerate direction (one view missing) -> report NaN, don't crash
   146	        return float('nan'), np.full(max_rank, float('nan'), dtype=np.float32), float('nan')
   147	    qf = F.normalize(qf, dim=1)
   148	    gf = F.normalize(gf, dim=1)
   149	    distmat = (2 - 2 * qf @ gf.t()).numpy()   # cosine distance
   150	    num_q, num_g = distmat.shape
   151	    max_rank = min(max_rank, num_g)
   152	    indices = np.argsort(distmat, axis=1)
   153	    matches = (g_pids[indices] == q_pids[:, np.newaxis]).astype(np.int32)
   154	
   155	    all_cmc, all_AP, all_INP = [], [], []
   156	    num_valid_q = 0
   157	    for q_idx in range(num_q):
   158	        q_pid = q_pids[q_idx]
   159	        q_camid = q_camids[q_idx]
   160	        order = indices[q_idx]
   161	        # junk: same pid AND same camid
   162	        remove = (g_pids[order] == q_pid) & (g_camids[order] == q_camid)
   163	        keep = np.invert(remove)
   164	
   165	        raw_cmc = matches[q_idx][keep]
   166	        if not np.any(raw_cmc):
   167	            continue   # this query has no ground-truth in (filtered) gallery
   168	        cmc = raw_cmc.cumsum()
   169	
   170	        # mINP
   171	        pos_idx = np.where(raw_cmc == 1)[0]
   172	        max_pos_idx = pos_idx[-1]
   173	        inp = cmc[max_pos_idx] / (max_pos_idx + 1.0)
   174	        all_INP.append(inp)
   175	
   176	        cmc_clip = cmc.copy()
   177	        cmc_clip[cmc_clip > 1] = 1
   178	        all_cmc.append(cmc_clip[:max_rank])
   179	        num_valid_q += 1
   180	
   181	        num_rel = raw_cmc.sum()
   182	        tmp_cmc = raw_cmc.cumsum()
   183	        tmp_cmc = [x / (i + 1.0) for i, x in enumerate(tmp_cmc)]
   184	        tmp_cmc = np.asarray(tmp_cmc) * raw_cmc
   185	        AP = tmp_cmc.sum() / num_rel
   186	        all_AP.append(AP)
   187	
   188	    if num_valid_q == 0:
   189	        return float('nan'), np.full(max_rank, float('nan'), dtype=np.float32), float('nan')
   190	    all_cmc = np.asarray(all_cmc).astype(np.float32).sum(0) / num_valid_q
   191	    mAP = float(np.mean(all_AP))
   192	    mINP = float(np.mean(all_INP))
   193	    return mAP, all_cmc, mINP
   194

   196	def build_eval_loader(samples, args):
   197	    tf = build_transforms(is_train=False, img_size=tuple(args.img_size))
   198	    ds = CARGOImageDataset(samples, tf)
   199	    return DataLoader(ds, batch_size=args.test_batch, shuffle=False,
   200	                      num_workers=args.workers, pin_memory=True)

   203	def run_cross_view_eval(model, dataset, args, device):
   204	    """Evaluate both cross-view directions: A->G and G->A. Returns dict of metrics."""
   205	    q_aerial = filter_by_view(dataset.query, 'Aerial')
   206	    q_ground = filter_by_view(dataset.query, 'Ground')
   207	    g_aerial = filter_by_view(dataset.gallery, 'Aerial')
   208	    g_ground = filter_by_view(dataset.gallery, 'Ground')
   209	
   210	    results = {}
   211	    # A->G : aerial query vs ground gallery
   212	    for tag, q, g in (('A->G', q_aerial, g_ground),
   213	                      ('G->A', q_ground, g_aerial)):
   214	        ql = build_eval_loader(q, args)
   215	        gl = build_eval_loader(g, args)
   216	        qf, qp, qc = extract_features(model, ql, device, args.use_afd)
   217	        gf, gp, gc = extract_features(model, gl, device, args.use_afd)
   218	        mAP, cmc, mINP = eval_market(qf, qp, qc, gf, gp, gc)
   219	        results[tag] = {'mAP': mAP * 100, 'R1': cmc[0] * 100,
   220	                        'R5': cmc[4] * 100 if len(cmc) > 4 else float('nan'),
   221	                        'mINP': mINP * 100}
   222	    return results
   223	
   224	
   225	def print_eval(epoch, results):
   226	    print(f"  ---- A<->G cross-view eval @ epoch {epoch} ----")
   227	    for tag in ('A->G', 'G->A'):
   228	        r = results[tag]
   229	        print(f"    [{tag}] mAP={r['mAP']:.2f}  R1={r['R1']:.2f}  "
   230	              f"R5={r['R5']:.2f}  mINP={r['mINP']:.2f}")
   231	    mean_map = (results['A->G']['mAP'] + results['G->A']['mAP']) / 2
   232	    mean_r1 = (results['A->G']['R1'] + results['G->A']['R1']) / 2
   233	    print(f"    [mean] mAP={mean_map:.2f}  R1={mean_r1:.2f}")
   234	    return mean_map

   255	    ap.add_argument('--weight_decay', type=float, default=5e-4)
   256	    ap.add_argument('--P', type=int, default=16)
   257	    ap.add_argument('--K', type=int, default=4)
   258	    ap.add_argument('--test_batch', type=int, default=128)
   259	    ap.add_argument('--workers', type=int, default=8)
   260	    ap.add_argument('--margin', type=float, default=0.3)
   261	    ap.add_argument('--label_smooth', type=float, default=0.1)
   262	    ap.add_argument('--eval_period', type=int, default=10)
   263	    ap.add_argument('--seed', type=int, default=1)
   264	    ap.add_argument('--no_amp', action='store_true', help='disable mixed precision')
   265	    # model / AFD switches
   266	    ap.add_argument('--last_stride', type=int, default=1)
   267	    ap.add_argument('--pool', default='gem', choices=['gem', 'avg'])
   268	    ap.add_argument('--use_afd', action='store_true')
   269	    ap.add_argument('--afd_router', type=int, default=1)
   270	    ap.add_argument('--afd_cvfc', type=int, default=1)
   271	    ap.add_argument('--afd_stage', default='layer1',
   272	                    choices=['stem', 'layer1', 'layer2'])
   273	    ap.add_argument('--router_cond_view', type=int, default=1)
   274	    ap.add_argument('--low_r', type=float, default=0.125)
   275	    ap.add_argument('--mid_r', type=float, default=0.30)
   276	    ap.add_argument('--high_drop_p', type=float, default=0.5)
   277	    # AFD loss weights
   278	    ap.add_argument('--w_cvfc', type=float, default=0.5,
   279	                    help='weight of cross-view counterfactual consistency loss')
   280	    args = ap.parse_args()
   281	    # normalize int-bool flags
   282	    args.afd_router = bool(args.afd_router)
   283	    args.afd_cvfc = bool(args.afd_cvfc)
   284	    args.router_cond_view = bool(args.router_cond_view)
   285	
   286	    set_seed(args.seed)
   287	    os.makedirs(args.out_dir, exist_ok=True)
   288	    device = 'cuda'
   289	    batch_size = args.P * args.K
   290	
   291	    print("=" * 70)
   292	    print("AFD-ReID training")
   293	    print(f"  use_afd={args.use_afd} router={args.afd_router} cvfc={args.afd_cvfc} "
   294	          f"stage={args.afd_stage} cond_view={args.router_cond_view}")
   295	    print(f"  bs={batch_size} (P={args.P} K={args.K}) lr={args.lr} "
   296	          f"epochs={args.epochs} warmup={args.warmup_epochs} amp={not args.no_amp}")
   297	    print(f"  out_dir={args.out_dir}")
   298	    print("=" * 70)
   299	
   300	    # data
   301	    dataset = CARGO(root=args.data_root, verbose=True)
   302	    train_tf = build_transforms(is_train=True, img_size=tuple(args.img_size))
   303	    train_set = CARGOImageDataset(dataset.train, train_tf)
   304	    sampler = RandomIdentitySampler(dataset.train, batch_size, args.K)
   305	    train_loader = DataLoader(train_set, batch_size=batch_size, sampler=sampler,
   306	                              num_workers=args.workers, pin_memory=True,
   307	                              drop_last=True)
   308	
   309	    # model
   310	    model = build_model(dataset.num_train_pids, args).to(device)
   311	
   312	    # losses
   313	    ce = CrossEntropyLabelSmooth(dataset.num_train_pids, args.label_smooth)
   314	    tri = TripletLoss(args.margin)
   315	
   316	    # optimizer
   317	    optimizer = torch.optim.AdamW(model.parameters(), lr=args.lr,
   318	                                  weight_decay=args.weight_decay)
   319	    scheduler = WarmupCosineLR(optimizer, args.warmup_epochs, args.epochs)
   320	    scaler = torch.amp.GradScaler('cuda', enabled=not args.no_amp)
   321	
   322	    view_map = {'Aerial': 0, 'Ground': 1}
   323	    best_map = -1.0
   324	    best_epoch = -1
   325	    n_iter_total = len(train_loader)
   326	
   327	    for epoch in range(1, args.epochs + 1):
   328	        model.train()
   329	        t0 = time.time()
   330	        meters = {'loss': 0.0, 'ce': 0.0, 'tri': 0.0, 'cvfc': 0.0, 'acc': 0.0}
   331	        seen = 0
   332	
   333	        for it, batch in enumerate(train_loader):
   334	            imgs = batch['img'].to(device, non_blocking=True)
   335	            labels = batch['pid'].to(device, non_blocking=True)
   336	            vidx = torch.tensor([view_map[v] for v in batch['view']],
   337	                                device=device) if args.use_afd else None
   338	
   339	            optimizer.zero_grad()
   340	            with torch.amp.autocast('cuda', enabled=not args.no_amp):
   341	                out = model(imgs, view_idx=vidx,
   342	                            return_cvfc=(args.use_afd and args.afd_cvfc))
   343	                logits = out['logits']
   344	                gfeat = out['global_feat']
   345	                loss_ce = ce(logits, labels)
   346	                loss_tri = tri(gfeat, labels)
   347	                loss = loss_ce + loss_tri
   348	
   349	                loss_cvfc = torch.zeros((), device=device)
   350	                if args.use_afd and args.afd_cvfc and 'cf_lowpass_bn' in out:
   351	                    bn = out['bn_feat'].detach()  # anchor on the real feature
   352	                    # consistency: real <-> low-pass counterfactual,
   353	                    #              real <-> high-band-dropout counterfactual
   354	                    lp = out['cf_lowpass_bn']
   355	                    hd = out['cf_highdrop_bn']
   356	                    loss_cvfc = (F.mse_loss(F.normalize(lp, dim=1),
   357	                                            F.normalize(bn, dim=1))
   358	                                 + F.mse_loss(F.normalize(hd, dim=1),
   359	                                              F.normalize(bn, dim=1)))
   360	                    loss = loss + args.w_cvfc * loss_cvfc
   361	
   362	            scaler.scale(loss).backward()
   363	            scaler.step(optimizer)
   364	            scaler.update()
   365	
   366	            bs = imgs.size(0)
   367	            seen += bs
   368	            meters['loss'] += loss.item() * bs
   369	            meters['ce'] += loss_ce.item() * bs
   370	            meters['tri'] += loss_tri.item() * bs
   371	            meters['cvfc'] += float(loss_cvfc) * bs
   372	            meters['acc'] += (logits.argmax(1) == labels).float().sum().item()
   373	
   374	            if (it + 1) % 50 == 0 or (it + 1) == n_iter_total:
   375	                lr = optimizer.param_groups[0]['lr']
   376	                print(f"Epoch[{epoch}] Iter[{it + 1}/{n_iter_total}] "
   377	                      f"Loss: {meters['loss'] / seen:.3f} "
   378	                      f"CE: {meters['ce'] / seen:.3f} "
   379	                      f"Tri: {meters['tri'] / seen:.3f} "
   380	                      f"CVFC: {meters['cvfc'] / seen:.4f} "

 succeeded in 0ms:
     1	# encoding: utf-8
     2	"""
     3	AFD-ReID model: ResNet50 backbone + GeM/avg pool + BNNeck + ID classifier.
     4	
     5	Baseline (use_afd=False) is a standard strong ReID baseline (Luo et al. BoT):
     6	    resnet50(IMAGENET1K_V1) -> last_stride=1 -> pool -> BNNeck -> linear classifier
     7	    train returns (global_feat, logits); test returns L2-normalized BN feature.
     8	
     9	AFD modules (use_afd=True) -- altitude-frequency decoupling, both switchable:
    10	  * Frequency Reliability Router (afd_router):
    11	      split a shallow feature map into low / mid / high frequency bands via 2D FFT,
    12	      predict per-band reliability weights with a light gate (optionally conditioned
    13	      on view/altitude), and recombine the bands. The recombined feature replaces the
    14	      input to the rest of the backbone. Designed to *down-weight unreliable high-freq*
    15	      content under aerial (low-resolution) views.
    16	  * Cross-View Frequency Counterfactual (afd_cvfc):
    17	      training-time regularizer (no test-time cost). Two switchable sub-mechanisms:
    18	        - high-band dropout: zero the high-freq band of the shallow feature with prob p,
    19	          producing a counterfactual view that must keep the same identity.
    20	        - low/high consistency: penalize the distance between the embedding of the full
    21	          feature and the embedding of its low-pass counterfactual, so identity relies
    22	          on the stable low/mid band shared across A<->G.
    23	      The CVFC forward exposes counterfactual features; the losses live in afd_train.py
    24	      so the mechanism stays cleanly ablatable.
    25	
    26	Every AFD piece is gated by flags so `use_afd=False` reproduces the baseline exactly
    27	(the AFD submodules are not even constructed when use_afd=False).
    28	"""
    29	import math
    30	
    31	import torch
    32	import torch.nn as nn
    33	import torch.nn.functional as F
    34	import torchvision
    35	
    36	
    37	# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
    38	# weight init (BoT style)
    39	# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
    40	def weights_init_kaiming(m):
    41	    classname = m.__class__.__name__
    42	    if classname.find('Linear') != -1:
    43	        nn.init.kaiming_normal_(m.weight, a=0, mode='fan_out')
    44	        if m.bias is not None:
    45	            nn.init.constant_(m.bias, 0.0)
    46	    elif classname.find('Conv') != -1:
    47	        nn.init.kaiming_normal_(m.weight, a=0, mode='fan_in')
    48	        if m.bias is not None:
    49	            nn.init.constant_(m.bias, 0.0)
    50	    elif classname.find('BatchNorm') != -1:
    51	        if m.affine:
    52	            nn.init.constant_(m.weight, 1.0)
    53	            nn.init.constant_(m.bias, 0.0)
    54	
    55	
    56	def weights_init_classifier(m):
    57	    classname = m.__class__.__name__
    58	    if classname.find('Linear') != -1:
    59	        nn.init.normal_(m.weight, std=0.001)
    60	        if m.bias is not None:
    61	            nn.init.constant_(m.bias, 0.0)
    62	
    63	
    64	# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
    65	# GeM pooling
    66	# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
    67	class GeMPool(nn.Module):
    68	    def __init__(self, p=3.0, eps=1e-6):
    69	        super().__init__()
    70	        self.p = nn.Parameter(torch.ones(1) * p)
    71	        self.eps = eps
    72	
    73	    def forward(self, x):
    74	        x = x.clamp(min=self.eps).pow(self.p)
    75	        x = F.adaptive_avg_pool2d(x, 1).pow(1.0 / self.p)
    76	        return x.flatten(1)
    77	
    78	
    79	# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
    80	# Frequency band decomposition (shared by Router and CVFC)
    81	# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
    82	def _band_masks(H, W, low_r=0.125, mid_r=0.30, device='cpu', dtype=torch.float32):
    83	    """Build (low, mid, high) centered rectangular FFT-shifted masks on an HxW grid.
    84	
    85	    low  : central box of half-size low_r*(H,W)
    86	    mid  : ring between low_r and mid_r
    87	    high : everything outside mid_r
    88	    Masks sum to 1 everywhere -> band partition is exact and lossless.
    89	    """
    90	    cy, cx = H // 2, W // 2
    91	    ry1, rx1 = max(1, int(H * low_r)), max(1, int(W * low_r))
    92	    ry2, rx2 = max(ry1 + 1, int(H * mid_r)), max(rx1 + 1, int(W * mid_r))
    93	
    94	    low = torch.zeros(H, W, device=device, dtype=dtype)
    95	    low[cy - ry1:cy + ry1, cx - rx1:cx + rx1] = 1.0
    96	
    97	    midbox = torch.zeros(H, W, device=device, dtype=dtype)
    98	    midbox[cy - ry2:cy + ry2, cx - rx2:cx + rx2] = 1.0
    99	    mid = midbox - low                     # ring
   100	    high = 1.0 - midbox                     # outside
   101	    return low, mid, high
   102	
   103	
   104	def decompose_bands(x, low_r=0.125, mid_r=0.30):
   105	    """Decompose a feature map x:(B,C,H,W) into (low, mid, high) spatial-domain maps.
   106	
   107	    Sum of the three equals x (up to fp error). Done per-sample via 2D FFT over (H,W).
   108	    """
   109	    B, C, H, W = x.shape
   110	    f = torch.fft.fftshift(torch.fft.fft2(x.float(), dim=(-2, -1)), dim=(-2, -1))
   111	    low_m, mid_m, high_m = _band_masks(H, W, low_r, mid_r, device=x.device)
   112	    out = []
   113	    for m in (low_m, mid_m, high_m):
   114	        fm = f * m.view(1, 1, H, W)
   115	        band = torch.fft.ifft2(torch.fft.ifftshift(fm, dim=(-2, -1)), dim=(-2, -1)).real
   116	        out.append(band.to(x.dtype))
   117	    return out  # [low, mid, high]
   118	
   119	
   120	# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
   121	# AFD Module 1: Frequency Reliability Router
   122	# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
   123	class FrequencyReliabilityRouter(nn.Module):
   124	    """Re-weight (low, mid, high) bands of a feature map by learned reliability.
   125	
   126	    weight = softmax over 3 bands, optionally conditioned on view (aerial/ground).
   127	    output = w_low*low + w_mid*mid + w_high*high   (residual-blended with input).
   128	
   129	    cond_on_view: if True, the gate input is concatenated with a learned per-view
   130	    embedding so aerial vs ground can learn different band reliabilities.
   131	    """
   132	
   133	    def __init__(self, channels, low_r=0.125, mid_r=0.30, cond_on_view=True,
   134	                 residual=True):
   135	        super().__init__()
   136	        self.low_r = low_r
   137	        self.mid_r = mid_r
   138	        self.cond_on_view = cond_on_view
   139	        self.residual = residual
   140	
   141	        gate_in = channels + (2 if cond_on_view else 0)
   142	        hidden = max(channels // 8, 16)
   143	        self.gate = nn.Sequential(
   144	            nn.Linear(gate_in, hidden),
   145	            nn.ReLU(inplace=True),
   146	            nn.Linear(hidden, 3),
   147	        )
   148	        if cond_on_view:
   149	            # view embedding: index 0 = Aerial, 1 = Ground
   150	            self.view_emb = nn.Embedding(2, 2)
   151	            nn.init.zeros_(self.view_emb.weight)
   152	        # init last gate layer near-zero so initial weights ~ uniform (stable start)
   153	        nn.init.zeros_(self.gate[-1].weight)
   154	        nn.init.zeros_(self.gate[-1].bias)
   155	
   156	    def forward(self, x, view_idx=None):
   157	        """x:(B,C,H,W); view_idx:(B,) long in {0,1} or None.
   158	
   159	        Returns (recombined feature, band_weights(B,3)).
   160	        """
   161	        low, mid, high = decompose_bands(x, self.low_r, self.mid_r)
   162	        ctx = F.adaptive_avg_pool2d(x, 1).flatten(1)          # (B,C)
   163	        if self.cond_on_view and view_idx is not None:
   164	            ctx = torch.cat([ctx, self.view_emb(view_idx)], dim=1)
   165	        elif self.cond_on_view:
   166	            # no view at hand (e.g. some test paths) -> zero pad
   167	            ctx = torch.cat([ctx, ctx.new_zeros(ctx.size(0), 2)], dim=1)
   168	        w = torch.softmax(self.gate(ctx), dim=1)              # (B,3)
   169	        wl, wm, wh = w[:, 0], w[:, 1], w[:, 2]
   170	
   171	        def b(weight, band):
   172	            return weight.view(-1, 1, 1, 1) * band
   173	
   174	        recomb = b(wl, low) + b(wm, mid) + b(wh, high)
   175	        # scale by 3 so that uniform weights (1/3 each) reproduce the input map
   176	        recomb = recomb * 3.0
   177	        if self.residual:
   178	            recomb = 0.5 * x + 0.5 * recomb
   179	        return recomb, w
   180	
   181	
   182	# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
   183	# AFD Module 2: Cross-View Frequency Counterfactual (training-time)
   184	# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
   185	class CrossViewFrequencyCounterfactual(nn.Module):
   186	    """Produce counterfactual feature maps for the consistency / dropout losses.
   187	
   188	    Pure functional band ops -- no learnable params; kept as a module so the
   189	    behavior is centralized and switchable. Used only in training.
   190	    """
   191	
   192	    def __init__(self, low_r=0.125, mid_r=0.30, high_drop_p=0.5):
   193	        super().__init__()
   194	        self.low_r = low_r
   195	        self.mid_r = mid_r
   196	        self.high_drop_p = high_drop_p
   197	
   198	    def high_band_dropout(self, x):
   199	        """Per-sample: with prob high_drop_p, drop the high band (keep low+mid)."""
   200	        low, mid, high = decompose_bands(x, self.low_r, self.mid_r)
   201	        B = x.size(0)
   202	        keep = (torch.rand(B, device=x.device) >= self.high_drop_p).float()
   203	        keep = keep.view(B, 1, 1, 1)
   204	        return low + mid + keep * high
   205	
   206	    def low_pass(self, x):
   207	        """Keep only low+mid band (a stable, view-invariant counterfactual)."""
   208	        low, mid, _ = decompose_bands(x, self.low_r, self.mid_r)
   209	        return low + mid
   210	
   211	
   212	# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
   213	# Main model
   214	# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
   215	class AFDModel(nn.Module):
   216	    def __init__(self, num_classes, last_stride=1, pool='gem',
   217	                 pretrained=True,
   218	                 use_afd=False,
   219	                 afd_router=True, afd_cvfc=True,
   220	                 afd_stage='layer1',
   221	                 router_cond_view=True,
   222	                 low_r=0.125, mid_r=0.30, high_drop_p=0.5):
   223	        super().__init__()
   224	        self.use_afd = use_afd
   225	        self.afd_router = use_afd and afd_router
   226	        self.afd_cvfc = use_afd and afd_cvfc
   227	        self.afd_stage = afd_stage
   228	        self.in_planes = 2048
   229	
   230	        weights = 'IMAGENET1K_V1' if pretrained else None
   231	        resnet = torchvision.models.resnet50(weights=weights)
   232	        # ReID standard: last block stride 1 -> larger spatial map (16x8 for 256x128)
   233	        if last_stride == 1:
   234	            resnet.layer4[0].conv2.stride = (1, 1)
   235	            resnet.layer4[0].downsample[0].stride = (1, 1)
   236	
   237	        # split backbone so the router can be inserted after a shallow stage
   238	        self.stem = nn.Sequential(
   239	            resnet.conv1, resnet.bn1, resnet.relu, resnet.maxpool)
   240	        self.layer1 = resnet.layer1   # 256 ch
   241	        self.layer2 = resnet.layer2   # 512 ch
   242	        self.layer3 = resnet.layer3   # 1024 ch
   243	        self.layer4 = resnet.layer4   # 2048 ch
   244	
   245	        # channel count at the chosen insertion stage
   246	        stage_ch = {'stem': 64, 'layer1': 256, 'layer2': 512}
   247	        assert afd_stage in stage_ch, f"afd_stage must be one of {list(stage_ch)}"
   248	        self.router_channels = stage_ch[afd_stage]
   249	
   250	        if self.afd_router:
   251	            self.router = FrequencyReliabilityRouter(
   252	                self.router_channels, low_r=low_r, mid_r=mid_r,
   253	                cond_on_view=router_cond_view)
   254	        if self.afd_cvfc:
   255	            self.cvfc = CrossViewFrequencyCounterfactual(
   256	                low_r=low_r, mid_r=mid_r, high_drop_p=high_drop_p)
   257	
   258	        # pooling
   259	        self.pool = GeMPool() if pool == 'gem' else None  # None -> avg in forward
   260	
   261	        # BNNeck
   262	        self.bottleneck = nn.BatchNorm1d(self.in_planes)
   263	        self.bottleneck.bias.requires_grad_(False)
   264	        self.bottleneck.apply(weights_init_kaiming)
   265	
   266	        self.classifier = nn.Linear(self.in_planes, num_classes, bias=False)
   267	        self.classifier.apply(weights_init_classifier)
   268	
   269	    # --- backbone forward with optional router insertion ------------------- #
   270	    def _forward_backbone(self, x, view_idx=None, feat_override=None,
   271	                          insert_router=False):
   272	        """Run stem->layer4. If insert_router, apply router at self.afd_stage.
   273	
   274	        feat_override: if given, a dict {stage: tensor} used to *replace* the
   275	        feature at that stage (for counterfactual passes that re-enter mid-network).
   276	        """
   277	        band_w = None
   278	        x = self.stem(x)
   279	        if self.afd_stage == 'stem':
   280	            x = self._maybe_route(x, 'stem', view_idx, insert_router)
   281	            x, band_w = x if isinstance(x, tuple) else (x, band_w)
   282	
   283	        x = self.layer1(x)
   284	        if self.afd_stage == 'layer1':
   285	            x = self._maybe_route(x, 'layer1', view_idx, insert_router)
   286	            x, band_w = x if isinstance(x, tuple) else (x, band_w)
   287	
   288	        x = self.layer2(x)
   289	        if self.afd_stage == 'layer2':
   290	            x = self._maybe_route(x, 'layer2', view_idx, insert_router)
   291	            x, band_w = x if isinstance(x, tuple) else (x, band_w)
   292	
   293	        x = self.layer3(x)
   294	        x = self.layer4(x)
   295	        return x, band_w
   296	
   297	    def _maybe_route(self, x, stage, view_idx, insert_router):
   298	        if insert_router and self.afd_router and stage == self.afd_stage:
   299	            return self.router(x, view_idx)   # returns (feat, w)
   300	        return x
   301	
   302	    def _pool(self, x):
   303	        if self.pool is not None:
   304	            return self.pool(x)
   305	        return F.adaptive_avg_pool2d(x, 1).flatten(1)
   306	
   307	    def _embed(self, x):
   308	        """global feat -> BNNeck feat."""
   309	        g = self._pool(x)
   310	        bn = self.bottleneck(g)
   311	        return g, bn
   312	
   313	    # --- public forward ---------------------------------------------------- #
   314	    def forward(self, x, view_idx=None, return_cvfc=False):
   315	        """
   316	        Train: returns dict with global_feat, bn_feat, logits, band_w,
   317	               and (if return_cvfc & afd_cvfc) counterfactual embeddings.
   318	        Eval : returns L2-normalized BN feature.
   319	        """
   320	        feat_map, band_w = self._forward_backbone(

exec

     2	"""
     3	CARGO (aerial-ground person ReID) torch Dataset + standard ReID transforms.
     4	
     5	Data layout (lab-3090):
     6	    /root/work/SOLIDER-REID/data/CARGO/{train,query,gallery}/Cam{1..13}/*.jpg
     7	Filename: Cam<N>_<time>_<pid>_<idx>.jpg   e.g. Cam13_day_376_26154.jpg
     8	    camid = int(basename.split('_')[0][3:])     # 1..13
     9	    pid   = int(basename.split('_')[2])
    10	    view  = 'Aerial' if camid <= 5 else 'Ground'   # cam1-5 aerial, cam6-13 ground
    11	
    12	Protocol (follows the official fast-reid CARGO parser in cargo.py, protocol-1 ALL):
    13	    - train: relabel pids to a contiguous [0, num_train_pids) range.
    14	    - query / gallery: keep ORIGINAL pids (test identities are disjoint from train).
    15	    - camid is kept 0-indexed (camid-1) to match fast-reid convention.
    16	
    17	The cross-view A<->G evaluation (the core confound test) is built downstream in
    18	afd_train.py / band_analysis.py by filtering query/gallery on `view`:
    19	    Aerial-as-query (A->G): query view==Aerial, gallery view==Ground
    20	    Ground-as-query (G->A): query view==Ground, gallery view==Aerial
    21	"""
    22	import os
    23	import glob
    24	import random
    25	import math
    26	
    27	import numpy as np
    28	from PIL import Image
    29	
    30	import torch
    31	from torch.utils.data import Dataset
    32	import torchvision.transforms as T
    33
