    35	# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
    36	# Dataset parsing
    37	# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
    38	def _parse_name(img_path):
    39	    """Return (pid, camid_1based, view) parsed from a CARGO filename."""
    40	    name = os.path.basename(img_path)
    41	    parts = name.split('_')
    42	    # parts = ['Cam13', 'day', '376', '26154.jpg']
    43	    camid = int(parts[0][3:])           # strip 'Cam' prefix -> 13
    44	    pid = int(parts[2])
    45	    view = 'Aerial' if camid <= 5 else 'Ground'
    46	    return pid, camid, view
    47	
    48	
    49	def _scan_split(split_dir):
    50	    """Glob Cam1..Cam13 under split_dir, return list of (path, pid, camid_1based, view)."""
    51	    img_paths = []
    52	    for cam_index in range(13):
    53	        img_paths += glob.glob(os.path.join(split_dir, f'Cam{cam_index + 1}', '*.jpg'))
    54	    data = []
    55	    for p in img_paths:
    56	        pid, camid, view = _parse_name(p)
    57	        data.append((p, pid, camid, view))
    58	    return data
    59	
    60	
    61	class CARGO(object):
    62	    """
    63	    Lightweight CARGO meta-dataset (fast-reid style, protocol-1 ALL).
    64	
    65	    Attributes (each a list of dicts with keys: img_path, pid, camid, view):
    66	        self.train, self.query, self.gallery
    67	    self.num_train_pids / num_train_imgs / num_train_cams available after build.
    68	    Train pids are relabeled to [0, num_train_pids); test pids keep original values.
    69	    """
    70	
    71	    def __init__(self, root='/root/work/SOLIDER-REID/data', verbose=True):
    72	        self.dataset_dir = os.path.join(root, 'CARGO')
    73	        self.train_dir = os.path.join(self.dataset_dir, 'train')
    74	        self.query_dir = os.path.join(self.dataset_dir, 'query')
    75	        self.gallery_dir = os.path.join(self.dataset_dir, 'gallery')
    76	
    77	        for d in (self.train_dir, self.query_dir, self.gallery_dir):
    78	            if not os.path.isdir(d):
    79	                raise RuntimeError(f"CARGO split dir not found: {d}")
    80	
    81	        train_raw = _scan_split(self.train_dir)
    82	        query_raw = _scan_split(self.query_dir)
    83	        gallery_raw = _scan_split(self.gallery_dir)
    84	
    85	        # relabel train pids to a contiguous range; keep test pids original.
    86	        train_pids = sorted({pid for _, pid, _, _ in train_raw})
    87	        self.pid2label = {pid: idx for idx, pid in enumerate(train_pids)}
    88	
    89	        self.train = self._pack(train_raw, relabel=True)
    90	        self.query = self._pack(query_raw, relabel=False)
    91	        self.gallery = self._pack(gallery_raw, relabel=False)
    92	
    93	        self.num_train_pids = len(train_pids)
    94	        self.num_train_imgs = len(self.train)
    95	        self.num_train_cams = len({d['camid'] for d in self.train})
    96	
    97	        if verbose:
    98	            self._print_stats()
    99	
   100	    def _pack(self, raw, relabel):
   101	        out = []
   102	        for path, pid, camid, view in raw:
   103	            label = self.pid2label[pid] if relabel else pid
   104	            out.append({
   105	                'img_path': path,
   106	                'pid': label,
   107	                'camid': camid - 1,   # 0-indexed, fast-reid convention
   108	                'view': view,
   109	            })
   110	        return out
   111	
   112	    def _print_stats(self):
   113	        def cnt(split):
   114	            pids = len({d['pid'] for d in split})
   115	            cams = len({d['camid'] for d in split})
   116	            a = sum(d['view'] == 'Aerial' for d in split)
   117	            g = sum(d['view'] == 'Ground' for d in split)
   118	            return len(split), pids, cams, a, g
   119	        print("=> CARGO loaded (protocol-1 ALL)")
   120	        print("  -----------------------------------------------------------")
   121	        print("  subset   | # imgs | # pids | # cams | aerial | ground")
   122	        print("  -----------------------------------------------------------")
   123	        for name, split in (('train', self.train), ('query', self.query),
   124	                            ('gallery', self.gallery)):
   125	            n, p, c, a, g = cnt(split)
   126	            print(f"  {name:8s} | {n:6d} | {p:6d} | {c:6d} | {a:6d} | {g:6d}")
   127	        print("  -----------------------------------------------------------")
   128	
   129	
   130	# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
   131	# Transforms
   132	# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
   133	def build_transforms(is_train, img_size=(256, 128), padding=10,
   134	                     re_prob=0.5, mean=(0.485, 0.456, 0.406),
   135	                     std=(0.229, 0.224, 0.225)):
   136	    """Standard ReID transforms.
   137	
   138	    Train: Resize -> HFlip -> Pad+RandomCrop -> ToTensor -> Normalize -> RandomErasing
   139	    Test : Resize -> ToTensor -> Normalize
   140	    """
   141	    normalize = T.Normalize(mean=mean, std=std)
   142	    if is_train:
   143	        return T.Compose([
   144	            T.Resize(img_size, interpolation=T.InterpolationMode.BICUBIC),
   145	            T.RandomHorizontalFlip(p=0.5),
   146	            T.Pad(padding),
   147	            T.RandomCrop(img_size),
   148	            T.ToTensor(),
   149	            normalize,
   150	            T.RandomErasing(p=re_prob, value=mean),
   151	        ])
   152	    return T.Compose([
   153	        T.Resize(img_size, interpolation=T.InterpolationMode.BICUBIC),
   154	        T.ToTensor(),
   155	        normalize,
   156	    ])
   157	
   158	
   159	# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
   160	# torch Dataset
   161	# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
   162	class CARGOImageDataset(Dataset):
   163	    """Wraps a list of sample dicts into an (img, pid, camid, view, path) Dataset."""
   164	
   165	    def __init__(self, samples, transform=None):
   166	        self.samples = samples
   167	        self.transform = transform
   168	
   169	    def __len__(self):
   170	        return len(self.samples)
   171	
   172	    def _read_image(self, path):
   173	        # robust loader (some synthetic frames can be flaky); never crash a worker.
   174	        got = None
   175	        for _ in range(5):
   176	            try:
   177	                got = Image.open(path).convert('RGB')
   178	                break
   179	            except (IOError, OSError):
   180	                continue
   181	        if got is None:
   182	            # last-resort black image so a single bad file doesn't kill training
   183	            got = Image.new('RGB', (128, 256))
   184	        return got
   185	
   186	    def __getitem__(self, idx):
   187	        s = self.samples[idx]
   188	        img = self._read_image(s['img_path'])
   189	        if self.transform is not None:
   190	            img = self.transform(img)
   191	        return {
   192	            'img': img,
   193	            'pid': s['pid'],
   194	            'camid': s['camid'],
   195	            'view': s['view'],
   196	            'img_path': s['img_path'],
   197	        }
   198	
   199	
   200	# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
   201	# PK sampler (P identities x K instances) for triplet mining
   202	# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
   203	class RandomIdentitySampler(torch.utils.data.Sampler):
   204	    """Sample batches of P identities, K instances each (default P=16, K=4 -> bs=64)."""
   205	
   206	    def __init__(self, samples, batch_size, num_instances):
   207	        self.samples = samples
   208	        self.batch_size = batch_size
   209	        self.num_instances = num_instances
   210	        self.num_pids_per_batch = batch_size // num_instances
   211	        assert batch_size % num_instances == 0, \
   212	            "batch_size must be divisible by num_instances"
   213	
   214	        self.index_by_pid = {}
   215	        for idx, s in enumerate(samples):
   216	            self.index_by_pid.setdefault(s['pid'], []).append(idx)
   217	        self.pids = list(self.index_by_pid.keys())
   218	
   219	        # estimate length: total usable images rounded to batch
   220	        self.length = 0
   221	        for pid in self.pids:
   222	            n = len(self.index_by_pid[pid])
   223	            n = max(n, self.num_instances)
   224	            self.length += n - n % self.num_instances
   225	
   226	    def __iter__(self):
   227	        batch_idxs_dict = {}
   228	        for pid in self.pids:
   229	            idxs = list(self.index_by_pid[pid])
   230	            if len(idxs) < self.num_instances:
   231	                idxs = np.random.choice(idxs, size=self.num_instances,
   232	                                        replace=True).tolist()
   233	            random.shuffle(idxs)
   234	            batches = []
   235	            batch = []
   236	            for i in idxs:
   237	                batch.append(i)
   238	                if len(batch) == self.num_instances:
   239	                    batches.append(batch)
   240	                    batch = []
   241	            batch_idxs_dict[pid] = batches
   242	
   243	        avail_pids = [pid for pid in self.pids if batch_idxs_dict[pid]]
   244	        final = []
   245	        while len(avail_pids) >= self.num_pids_per_batch:
   246	            selected = random.sample(avail_pids, self.num_pids_per_batch)
   247	            for pid in selected:
   248	                final.extend(batch_idxs_dict[pid].pop(0))
   249	                if not batch_idxs_dict[pid]:
   250	                    avail_pids.remove(pid)
   251	        self.length = len(final)
   252	        return iter(final)
   253	
   254	    def __len__(self):
   255	        return self.length
   256	
   257	
   258	# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
   259	# Eval-set view filtering helpers (cross-view A<->G splits)
   260	# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
   261	def filter_by_view(samples, view):
   262	    """Return subset whose 'view' == view ('Aerial' or 'Ground')."""
   263	    return [s for s in samples if s['view'] == view]
   264

experiments/cargo_cvpb/codex_review_raw.txt:3675:experiments/cargo_cvpb/claude_review.md:16:1. **训练动力学风险(High×2)**：早期 epoch OVP loss 会**结构性退化为零梯度**(冷启动稀疏 + 单列有效 → CE=0)，同时 `tau=0.05` 在随机原型上会给出**尖锐梯度尖峰**。两者叠加 → OVP 的 `meters['ovp']` 早期打印 ~0 不是收敛而是退化，监控会被误导，且 λ 早期实际不起作用。这不是 bug，但会让 kill-switch #2("ep10/20 ≥+1.0")的判读失真。

experiments/cargo_cvpb/codex_review_raw.txt:3801:2. **novelty 撞车(High，方法稿层面)**：子代理联网查重判定 **likely-prior-art-collision**。"per-view identity EMA prototype + opposite-view InfoNCE"与 **CMPC(CVPR22)** 近乎逐字同构(只是 voice-face→aerial-ground、无监督→有监督)，且 **PDPA(2025, 同 CARGO)** 已占用"aerial-ground 上 prototype alignment"的命名与 framing。作为 empirical 涨点模块没问题；作为 headline 贡献过不了 novelty review。
experiments/cargo_cvpb/codex_review_raw.txt:3817:- **机制**: 早期 bank 稀疏(每 pid 每 view 需先被见过才 inited)。经 `own_ok` 过滤后，若某样本的 own-opp 原型存在但**其它 opp 原型几乎都未初始化**，logit 行被 `-inf` 主导；极端只剩 own 一列有效时 `cross_entropy` 在单类上 softmax=1 → **loss≈0 → 零梯度**。我本地脚本已验证此路径(单有效列 CE=0.0，不是 NaN)。
experiments/cargo_cvpb/codex_review_raw.txt:3873:`update`(L94-111)外层按 view 循环，pid 同时出现在两视角时 `bank[pid,0]`/`bank[pid,1]` 独立写；`fv[lv==pid].mean(0)` 只平均同视角同 pid。**无 cross-view 污染**。
experiments/cargo_cvpb/codex_review_raw.txt:3910:- ★framing(避开那堵墙): **不是"模型被 view confound 骗"**(那已被 trained 模型 handle)——而是 **aerial-ground 单张图的身份证据在两视角下不一一对应**, 这是 ID loss/triplet 的全局监督约束不到的真实 gap(局部对应/跨视角证据补全)。
experiments/cargo_cvpb/codex_review_raw.txt:3916:- **模块1 OVP-Mem(Opposite-View Prototype Memory)**: 每 train pid 维护 aerial / ground 两个 EMA 原型。样本除 CE+triplet 外, aerial image 拉近同 pid 的 **ground** 原型(InfoNCE: sim(z, P[y, opp_view])/τ 分类到 y)。≠死掉的 CV-triplet(那是 batch 内跨视角正对稀缺假设, 已证 88% batch 有; 这里是**全局原型级跨视角监督, 覆盖 hard tail**)。
experiments/cargo_cvpb/codex_review_raw.txt:3929:- VDT(view decoupling + orthogonal): 我们不做 view-related/unrelated 减法解耦。
experiments/cargo_cvpb/codex_review_raw.txt:3932:- ViSA(view-aware expert graph): 不做 expert/graph。
experiments/cargo_cvpb/codex_review_raw.txt:3933:- SD-ReID(diffusion 生成 all-view): 不用 diffusion。
experiments/cargo_cvpb/codex_review_raw.txt:3934:- 我们的: **cross-view local evidence densification via opposite-view prototype + non-correspondence set matching**。

experiments/cargo_cvpb/codex_review_raw.txt:4323:experiments/cargo_cvpb/codex_review_raw.txt:3816:- **位置**: `afd_train.py` `OVPMemory.loss` L124-148；冷启动门控 L130-139；`tau` 默认 L190。

experiments/cargo_cvpb/codex_review_raw.txt:4325:experiments/cargo_cvpb/codex_review_raw.txt:3819:- **另一面**: `tau=0.05` 把 cos∈[-1,1] 拉到 logits∈[-20,20]。当 ≥2 列有效且原型(EMA of 1-4 样本，L103)还很 noisy 时，若 own 不是 argmax，CE 梯度极尖 → 训练初期对 encoder 注入大梯度。整体 OVP 行为**双峰**(退化≈0 / 尖峰)。

experiments/cargo_cvpb/codex_review_raw.txt:4364:experiments/cargo_cvpb/claude_review.md:12:OVP-Mem 的工程实现是**正确且不会崩**的：无 NaN/inf 路径、梯度隔离干净(bank 是 buffer 不回传)、dtype 一致、`--ovp` off 精确复现 baseline。kill-switch #1(maxsim probe)已 PASS(+0.86)且 pipeline 复现 32.48 准确。
experiments/cargo_cvpb/codex_review_raw.txt:4365:experiments/cargo_cvpb/claude_review.md:16:1. **训练动力学风险(High×2)**：早期 epoch OVP loss 会**结构性退化为零梯度**(冷启动稀疏 + 单列有效 → CE=0)，同时 `tau=0.05` 在随机原型上会给出**尖锐梯度尖峰**。两者叠加 → OVP 的 `meters['ovp']` 早期打印 ~0 不是收敛而是退化，监控会被误导，且 λ 早期实际不起作用。这不是 bug，但会让 kill-switch #2("ep10/20 ≥+1.0")的判读失真。
experiments/cargo_cvpb/codex_review_raw.txt:4366:experiments/cargo_cvpb/claude_review.md:17:2. **novelty 撞车(High，方法稿层面)**：子代理联网查重判定 **likely-prior-art-collision**。"per-view identity EMA prototype + opposite-view InfoNCE"与 **CMPC(CVPR22)** 近乎逐字同构(只是 voice-face→aerial-ground、无监督→有监督)，且 **PDPA(2025, 同 CARGO)** 已占用"aerial-ground 上 prototype alignment"的命名与 framing。作为 empirical 涨点模块没问题；作为 headline 贡献过不了 novelty review。
experiments/cargo_cvpb/codex_review_raw.txt:4367:experiments/cargo_cvpb/claude_review.md:31:### H1. OVP loss 早期结构性零梯度 + tau=0.05 尖峰（训练动力学，必须处理）
experiments/cargo_cvpb/codex_review_raw.txt:4368:experiments/cargo_cvpb/claude_review.md:32:- **位置**: `afd_train.py` `OVPMemory.loss` L124-148；冷启动门控 L130-139；`tau` 默认 L190。
experiments/cargo_cvpb/codex_review_raw.txt:4369:experiments/cargo_cvpb/claude_review.md:34:- **后果**: 第 1(几)个 epoch OVP 实际不产生有效监督，但 `meters['ovp']`(L292/301)打印接近 0，**会被误读为"已收敛"**；kill-switch #2 的"ep10 ≥+1.0"判读因此失真。
experiments/cargo_cvpb/codex_review_raw.txt:4370:experiments/cargo_cvpb/claude_review.md:35:- **另一面**: `tau=0.05` 把 cos∈[-1,1] 拉到 logits∈[-20,20]。当 ≥2 列有效且原型(EMA of 1-4 样本，L103)还很 noisy 时，若 own 不是 argmax，CE 梯度极尖 → 训练初期对 encoder 注入大梯度。整体 OVP 行为**双峰**(退化≈0 / 尖峰)。

experiments/cargo_cvpb/codex_review_raw.txt:4802:./experiments/cargo_cvpb/codex_review_raw.txt:3796:OVP-Mem 的工程实现是**正确且不会崩**的：无 NaN/inf 路径、梯度隔离干净(bank 是 buffer 不回传)、dtype 一致、`--ovp` off 精确复现 baseline。kill-switch #1(maxsim probe)已 PASS(+0.86)且 pipeline 复现 32.48 准确。
experiments/cargo_cvpb/codex_review_raw.txt:4803:./experiments/cargo_cvpb/codex_review_raw.txt:3800:1. **训练动力学风险(High×2)**：早期 epoch OVP loss 会**结构性退化为零梯度**(冷启动稀疏 + 单列有效 → CE=0)，同时 `tau=0.05` 在随机原型上会给出**尖锐梯度尖峰**。两者叠加 → OVP 的 `meters['ovp']` 早期打印 ~0 不是收敛而是退化，监控会被误导，且 λ 早期实际不起作用。这不是 bug，但会让 kill-switch #2("ep10/20 ≥+1.0")的判读失真。
experiments/cargo_cvpb/codex_review_raw.txt:4804:./experiments/cargo_cvpb/codex_review_raw.txt:3801:2. **novelty 撞车(High，方法稿层面)**：子代理联网查重判定 **likely-prior-art-collision**。"per-view identity EMA prototype + opposite-view InfoNCE"与 **CMPC(CVPR22)** 近乎逐字同构(只是 voice-face→aerial-ground、无监督→有监督)，且 **PDPA(2025, 同 CARGO)** 已占用"aerial-ground 上 prototype alignment"的命名与 framing。作为 empirical 涨点模块没问题；作为 headline 贡献过不了 novelty review。
experiments/cargo_cvpb/codex_review_raw.txt:4805:./experiments/cargo_cvpb/codex_review_raw.txt:3815:### H1. OVP loss 早期结构性零梯度 + tau=0.05 尖峰（训练动力学，必须处理）
experiments/cargo_cvpb/codex_review_raw.txt:4806:./experiments/cargo_cvpb/codex_review_raw.txt:3816:- **位置**: `afd_train.py` `OVPMemory.loss` L124-148；冷启动门控 L130-139；`tau` 默认 L190。
experiments/cargo_cvpb/codex_review_raw.txt:4807:./experiments/cargo_cvpb/codex_review_raw.txt:3818:- **后果**: 第 1(几)个 epoch OVP 实际不产生有效监督，但 `meters['ovp']`(L292/301)打印接近 0，**会被误读为"已收敛"**；kill-switch #2 的"ep10 ≥+1.0"判读因此失真。
experiments/cargo_cvpb/codex_review_raw.txt:4808:./experiments/cargo_cvpb/codex_review_raw.txt:3819:- **另一面**: `tau=0.05` 把 cos∈[-1,1] 拉到 logits∈[-20,20]。当 ≥2 列有效且原型(EMA of 1-4 样本，L103)还很 noisy 时，若 own 不是 argmax，CE 梯度极尖 → 训练初期对 encoder 注入大梯度。整体 OVP 行为**双峰**(退化≈0 / 尖峰)。
experiments/cargo_cvpb/codex_review_raw.txt:4809:./experiments/cargo_cvpb/codex_review_raw.txt:3822:  b. **日志增强**：每 iter 额外打印"本 iter 真正贡献 OVP 的样本数 / 有效 opp 列数"，让 monitor.md 能区分"退化 0"与"收敛 0"。按本仓库铁律"日志必须够重(能观察模块塌缩/过强/过弱)"，这一条**强制**。
experiments/cargo_cvpb/codex_review_raw.txt:4810:./experiments/cargo_cvpb/codex_review_raw.txt:3828:### M1. autocast 可能把 OVP matmul 下溢到 fp16，注释"fp32 for numerical safety"不成立
experiments/cargo_cvpb/codex_review_raw.txt:4811:./experiments/cargo_cvpb/codex_review_raw.txt:3832:- **建议**: 要么把 OVP loss 包进 `with torch.amp.autocast('cuda', enabled=False):`(在 `.float()` 之后)真正走 fp32；要么把注释改成"autocast 下 matmul 可能 fp16，但无溢出"，不要给读代码的人错误保证。
experiments/cargo_cvpb/codex_review_raw.txt:4812:./experiments/cargo_cvpb/codex_review_raw.txt:3839:### M3. OVP loss 用 `total/count` 按"视角组"等权，而非按 batch 样本均值
experiments/cargo_cvpb/codex_review_raw.txt:4813:./experiments/cargo_cvpb/codex_review_raw.txt:3842:- **评级**: 这看起来是有意的对称设计(两视角方向平权)，可接受，但与朴素"对所有 OVP 贡献样本求均值"不同。**建议在 design.md/注释中显式声明**这是 per-direction 平权，避免日后对 λ 标定产生困惑。
experiments/cargo_cvpb/codex_review_raw.txt:4814:./experiments/cargo_cvpb/codex_review_raw.txt:3852:  - **CMPC(CVPR22)**：per-cluster per-modality 原型 + momentum + InfoNCE 把实例拉向**对侧模态**同身份原型、推开其它——与 OVP-Mem loss 近乎逐字同构。差异仅 voice-face↔aerial-ground、无监督↔有监督。
experiments/cargo_cvpb/codex_review_raw.txt:4815:./experiments/cargo_cvpb/codex_review_raw.txt:3853:  - **MBCE(AAAI23, VI-ReID)**：modality-aware centroid proxies + momentum memory + cross-modality 对齐——同构，且已在 person ReID 发表。
experiments/cargo_cvpb/codex_review_raw.txt:4816:./experiments/cargo_cvpb/codex_review_raw.txt:3854:  - **Hetero-Center loss(VI-ReID)**：per-id per-modality center 对齐的 L2 形式；OVP-Mem 是其 InfoNCE 形式。
experiments/cargo_cvpb/codex_review_raw.txt:4817:./experiments/cargo_cvpb/codex_review_raw.txt:3855:  - **PDPA(2025, 同 CARGO)**：已声称"aerial-ground 上 per-perspective prototype alignment + memory bank"，**命名与 framing 已被占**(机制不同：prompt 驱动 + 可学习原型 + 中间空间 bank，故非完全相同)。
experiments/cargo_cvpb/codex_review_raw.txt:4818:./experiments/cargo_cvpb/codex_review_raw.txt:3856:- **后果**: design 自我辩护的"global prototype 覆盖 hard tail，不同于 batch CV-triplet"是**通用 memory-bank-vs-batch 论证**(MoCo/SpCL/Cluster-Contrast 以来的标准动机)，**不构成机制 novelty**。
experiments/cargo_cvpb/codex_review_raw.txt:4819:./experiments/cargo_cvpb/codex_review_raw.txt:3857:- **要求**: 这是 empirical 探索，跑可以；但**进方法稿前**(design.md L19 的"≥35-36→进方法稿")必须把 OVP-Mem **降级为 auxiliary loss**，真正的 novelty 由别处承担（子代理建议看 Module-2 non-correspondence local set-matching，撞车面更小）。**否则违反 CLAUDE.md 创新门槛 + "不在旧 branch 堆小模块"**。建议在 design.md 显式记一句"OVP-Mem 已知与 CMPC/MBCE/PDPA 高度重叠，仅作 empirical 组件，不作 headline"。
experiments/cargo_cvpb/codex_review_raw.txt:4820:./experiments/cargo_cvpb/codex_review_raw.txt:3870:ovp=False 时 `ovp=None`、`OVPMemory` **根本不构造**，forward 调用、`loss=ce+tri`、optimizer.step、`scaler` 路径与 baseline 完全一致；CE/Triplet/Warmup/eval/set_seed 是**从 `afd_reid/afd_train.py` import 的同一份**(byte-identical by construction)。baseline 可复现性不破坏。
experiments/cargo_cvpb/codex_review_raw.txt:4821:./experiments/cargo_cvpb/codex_review_raw.txt:3875:### L5.（已确认正确）optimizer 无需加 OVP 参数

experiments/cargo_cvpb/codex_review_raw.txt:4823:./experiments/cargo_cvpb/codex_review_raw.txt:3879:`update` L104 `gmean=fv[lv==pid].mean(0)` 若同 pid K=4 样本方向高度发散，pre-normalize 均值可能 small-norm，eps=1e-12 下放大噪声。但 EMA(L109，momentum 0.2 混两个单位向量)最小范数≈0.6，**真 div-by-zero 不可达**。trained 特征下概率极低，仅记录。

experiments/cargo_cvpb/design.md:7:- ★framing(避开那堵墙): **不是"模型被 view confound 骗"**(那已被 trained 模型 handle)——而是 **aerial-ground 单张图的身份证据在两视角下不一一对应**, 这是 ID loss/triplet 的全局监督约束不到的真实 gap(局部对应/跨视角证据补全)。
experiments/cargo_cvpb/design.md:13:- **模块1 OVP-Mem(Opposite-View Prototype Memory)**: 每 train pid 维护 aerial / ground 两个 EMA 原型。样本除 CE+triplet 外, aerial image 拉近同 pid 的 **ground** 原型(InfoNCE: sim(z, P[y, opp_view])/τ 分类到 y)。≠死掉的 CV-triplet(那是 batch 内跨视角正对稀缺假设, 已证 88% batch 有; 这里是**全局原型级跨视角监督, 覆盖 hard tail**)。
experiments/cargo_cvpb/design.md:26:- VDT(view decoupling + orthogonal): 我们不做 view-related/unrelated 减法解耦。
experiments/cargo_cvpb/design.md:29:- ViSA(view-aware expert graph): 不做 expert/graph。
experiments/cargo_cvpb/design.md:30:- SD-ReID(diffusion 生成 all-view): 不用 diffusion。
experiments/cargo_cvpb/design.md:31:- 我们的: **cross-view local evidence densification via opposite-view prototype + non-correspondence set matching**。
experiments/cargo_cvpb/design.md:39:- **双审**: Claude review 无 Critical, **H1 训练动力学已修**(λ warmup `ovp_warmup=10` + inited 列数日志, 防冷启动梯度尖峰), 审查通过。⚠️**H2/M5 novelty 撞车**: OVP-Mem(对侧视角原型+InfoNCE)≈ **PDPA(2025 同 CARGO)/ CMPC(CVPR22)/ MBCE(AAAI23 VI-ReID)** → **OVP 当组件不当 headline, 用 Local-MaxSim 集合匹配(non-correspondence)差异化**。Codex review 进行中。

 succeeded in 0ms:
   314	    def forward(self, x, view_idx=None, return_cvfc=False):
   315	        """
   316	        Train: returns dict with global_feat, bn_feat, logits, band_w,
   317	               and (if return_cvfc & afd_cvfc) counterfactual embeddings.
   318	        Eval : returns L2-normalized BN feature.
   319	        """
   320	        feat_map, band_w = self._forward_backbone(
   321	            x, view_idx=view_idx, insert_router=self.afd_router)
   322	        global_feat, bn_feat = self._embed(feat_map)
   323	
   324	        if not self.training:
   325	            return F.normalize(bn_feat, dim=1)
   326	
   327	        out = {
   328	            'global_feat': global_feat,   # for triplet (before BN)
   329	            'bn_feat': bn_feat,           # BN feature
   330	            'logits': self.classifier(bn_feat),
   331	            'band_w': band_w,             # (B,3) or None
   332	        }
   333	
   334	        if return_cvfc and self.afd_cvfc:
   335	            # build counterfactual at the SHALLOW stage, then run remainder.
   336	            # We re-extract the shallow feature, apply CVFC, and finish forward.
   337	            cf = self._forward_counterfactual(x, view_idx)
   338	            out.update(cf)
   339	        return out
   340	
   341	    def _forward_counterfactual(self, x, view_idx):
   342	        """Run two cheap counterfactual passes (high-drop, low-pass) from the
   343	        shallow stage onward, returning their BN embeddings for consistency loss."""
   344	        # get shallow feature up to (and including) the insertion stage
   345	        stage = self.afd_stage
   346	        shallow = self.stem(x)
   347	        if stage in ('layer1', 'layer2'):
   348	            shallow = self.layer1(shallow)
   349	        if stage == 'layer2':
   350	            shallow = self.layer2(shallow)
   351	
   352	        def finish(feat):
   353	            # continue from the stage AFTER the insertion point
   354	            if stage == 'stem':
   355	                h = self.layer1(feat); h = self.layer2(h)
   356	            elif stage == 'layer1':
   357	                h = self.layer2(feat)
   358	            else:  # layer2
   359	                h = feat
   360	            h = self.layer3(h); h = self.layer4(h)
   361	            _, bn = self._embed(h)
   362	            return bn
   363	
   364	        hd = self.cvfc.high_band_dropout(shallow)
   365	        lp = self.cvfc.low_pass(shallow)
   366	        return {
   367	            'cf_highdrop_bn': finish(hd),
   368	            'cf_lowpass_bn': finish(lp),
   369	        }
   370	
   371	
   372	def build_model(num_classes, args):
   373	    """Factory from an argparse-like namespace."""
   374	    return AFDModel(
   375	        num_classes=num_classes,
   376	        last_stride=getattr(args, 'last_stride', 1),
   377	        pool=getattr(args, 'pool', 'gem'),
   378	        pretrained=True,
   379	        use_afd=getattr(args, 'use_afd', False),
   380	        afd_router=getattr(args, 'afd_router', True),
   381	        afd_cvfc=getattr(args, 'afd_cvfc', True),
   382	        afd_stage=getattr(args, 'afd_stage', 'layer1'),
   383	        router_cond_view=getattr(args, 'router_cond_view', True),
   384	        low_r=getattr(args, 'low_r', 0.125),
   385	        mid_r=getattr(args, 'mid_r', 0.30),
   386	        high_drop_p=getattr(args, 'high_drop_p', 0.5),
   387	    )

/bin/zsh -lc "nl -ba experiments/cargo_cvpb/design.md | sed -n '1,140p'" in /Users/abdslm/Desktop/SOLIDER-REID
 succeeded in 0ms:
     1	# 实验 CVPB: CARGO 航拍-地面 跨视角原型桥接 + 局部证据匹配
     2	
     3	> 战略重思后选定(5 死角后, 3 codex strat 强收敛)。★★方法论转向: **放弃"验证 confound 是真依赖"(杀光一切), 改 empirical 涨点导向**——在 CARGO baseline 上试 +module 能否 beat = 真 kill-switch。这是 167 篇库真实配方(命名 gap + 2 模块 + 多数据集 SOTA 表 + framing)。
     4	
     5	## 动机
     6	- CARGO 航拍-地面 baseline(resnet50 BoT)mean A↔G mAP **32.48%**, VDT SOTA ~50% → **大 headroom**。AGPReID 活赛道(VDT CVPR24 / SeCap CVPR25 / GSAlign NeurIPS25 / ViSA CVPR26), 范式都是"命名视角/几何/语义 gap + 2 模块 + CARGO/AG-ReID 表", **不证 confound 依赖**。
     7	- ★framing(避开那堵墙): **不是"模型被 view confound 骗"**(那已被 trained 模型 handle)——而是 **aerial-ground 单张图的身份证据在两视角下不一一对应**, 这是 ID loss/triplet 的全局监督约束不到的真实 gap(局部对应/跨视角证据补全)。
     8	
     9	## 核心假设(empirical, 非 confound)
    10	全局 cosine 之外加 (1) 跨视角原型对齐 + (2) 局部 token 集合匹配, 能补全单图缺失的对侧视角证据 → mAP 涨。**判据 = 涨点, 不是 confound 真伪**。
    11	
    12	## 技术方案
    13	- **模块1 OVP-Mem(Opposite-View Prototype Memory)**: 每 train pid 维护 aerial / ground 两个 EMA 原型。样本除 CE+triplet 外, aerial image 拉近同 pid 的 **ground** 原型(InfoNCE: sim(z, P[y, opp_view])/τ 分类到 y)。≠死掉的 CV-triplet(那是 batch 内跨视角正对稀缺假设, 已证 88% batch 有; 这里是**全局原型级跨视角监督, 覆盖 hard tail**)。
    14	- **模块2 Local Token MaxSim**: feature map 切 K 个局部 evidence token, 测试 `global cosine + β·双向 MaxSim(tokens)`, 训练 token-level ID/SupCon + diversity 防坍缩。**不强制同部位对齐**(non-correspondence set matching)。≠GSAlign(无 TPS warp)/ ViSA(无 expert graph)/ SeCap(无 prompt)。
    15	
    16	## ★ kill-switch(empirical, 分阶段, 现成 baseline)
    17	1. **零训练 token-MaxSim probe**(最便宜, 先跑): CARGO baseline checkpoint 抽 feature map, 测 `global cosine` vs `global + 局部 MaxSim hybrid` 的 A↔G mAP。**+0.5~1.0 mAP** = 局部匹配有料 → 继续。
    18	2. **OVP-Mem only 训练**: ep10/20 vs baseline 同 epoch, **≥+1.0** 继续。
    19	3. **OVP+Local 训练**: final **≥35-36 mAP**(baseline 32.48)→ 进方法稿; 接近 38+ → 扩 Swin/VDT 级主表。
    20	- **不涨 = 换 module**(empirical, 不纠结 confound 真伪)。这是新 approach 的核心: 直接试涨点。
    21	
    22	## 对照 / 消融
    23	baseline 32.48 → +OVP-Mem → +Local-MaxSim → +both; K(token 数) / β / τ 敏感性; EMA momentum。
    24	
    25	## novelty 切开(查重)
    26	- VDT(view decoupling + orthogonal): 我们不做 view-related/unrelated 减法解耦。
    27	- SeCap(adaptive prompt + local refine): 不做 prompt recalibration。
    28	- GSAlign(LTPS 几何 warp + visibility mask): 不做 TPS / 不叫 visibility mask。
    29	- ViSA(view-aware expert graph): 不做 expert/graph。
    30	- SD-ReID(diffusion 生成 all-view): 不用 diffusion。
    31	- 我们的: **cross-view local evidence densification via opposite-view prototype + non-correspondence set matching**。
    32	
    33	## 数据 / venue
    34	- CARGO(lab-3090 现成)。补 AG-ReID.v2 多数据集表。
    35	- venue: AGPReID 活, ICME/ACCV/BMVC 稳, 强则冲 CVPR-tier 子方向。
    36	
    37	## ★ kill-switch 进度(2026-06-22)
    38	- **#1 零训练 MaxSim probe: 弱 PASS +0.86**(8×4 grid β=1.0 → 33.34 vs baseline 32.48; pipeline 验证 global cosine 精确复现 32.48; 增益集中 aerial-query A→G 32.90→34.18 **+1.28**, G→A 平~32)。局部 token 集合匹配有料但弱。
    39	- **双审**: Claude review 无 Critical, **H1 训练动力学已修**(λ warmup `ovp_warmup=10` + inited 列数日志, 防冷启动梯度尖峰), 审查通过。⚠️**H2/M5 novelty 撞车**: OVP-Mem(对侧视角原型+InfoNCE)≈ **PDPA(2025 同 CARGO)/ CMPC(CVPR22)/ MBCE(AAAI23 VI-ReID)** → **OVP 当组件不当 headline, 用 Local-MaxSim 集合匹配(non-correspondence)差异化**。Codex review 进行中。
    40	- **#2 OVP-Mem 训练**: 待 Codex 审通过 → 训 60ep, 看是否 beat 32.48(★empirical 判据, 非 confound)。ep10/20 看 warmup 后趋势, final ≥35 进方法线。
    41	
    42	## 备选(此线不涨则转)
    43	- strat_3: Camera Residual TTA(source-free open-camera test-time adaptation, Market→MSMT/CARGO OOD, 贴 DART3/TEMP)——OOD 方向, 也绕开那堵墙。
    44	- strat_1: CARGO View-Conditioned Token Evidence Matching(和本方案 module2 同源)。

experiments/cargo_cvpb/codex_review_raw.txt:4321:experiments/cargo_cvpb/codex_review_raw.txt:3801:2. **novelty 撞车(High，方法稿层面)**：子代理联网查重判定 **likely-prior-art-collision**。"per-view identity EMA prototype + opposite-view InfoNCE"与 **CMPC(CVPR22)** 近乎逐字同构(只是 voice-face→aerial-ground、无监督→有监督)，且 **PDPA(2025, 同 CARGO)** 已占用"aerial-ground 上 prototype alignment"的命名与 framing。作为 empirical 涨点模块没问题；作为 headline 贡献过不了 novelty review。

experiments/cargo_cvpb/codex_review_raw.txt:4391:experiments/cargo_cvpb/afd_train.py:16:    Maintain, per train pid, two EMA prototypes (aerial / ground) of the
experiments/cargo_cvpb/codex_review_raw.txt:4392:experiments/cargo_cvpb/afd_train.py:18:    [num_pid, 2, feat_dim]; EMA momentum 0.2.  Each step:
experiments/cargo_cvpb/codex_review_raw.txt:4393:experiments/cargo_cvpb/afd_train.py:19:      1) update the prototypes of the pids/views present in the batch (EMA),
experiments/cargo_cvpb/codex_review_raw.txt:4394:experiments/cargo_cvpb/afd_train.py:20:      2) add an InfoNCE loss pulling each sample toward its OWN pid's
experiments/cargo_cvpb/codex_review_raw.txt:4395:experiments/cargo_cvpb/afd_train.py:21:         OPPOSITE-view prototype and away from all other pids' opposite-view
experiments/cargo_cvpb/codex_review_raw.txt:4396:experiments/cargo_cvpb/afd_train.py:22:         prototypes:  CE( cos(z, P[:, opp_view]) / tau ,  y ).

experiments/cargo_cvpb/codex_review_raw.txt:4398:experiments/cargo_cvpb/afd_train.py:25:    Cold-start handling: a sample only contributes to the OVP loss once its own
experiments/cargo_cvpb/codex_review_raw.txt:4399:experiments/cargo_cvpb/afd_train.py:26:    opposite-view prototype has been initialized (seen >=1 time); candidate
experiments/cargo_cvpb/codex_review_raw.txt:4400:experiments/cargo_cvpb/afd_train.py:27:    columns that are still uninitialized are masked out of the InfoNCE logits so
experiments/cargo_cvpb/codex_review_raw.txt:4401:experiments/cargo_cvpb/afd_train.py:28:    a zero prototype can never act as an easy negative/positive.

experiments/cargo_cvpb/codex_review_raw.txt:5168:- **High**: OVP cold-start can look like “loss converged” while producing no useful gradient. In [afd_train.py](/Users/abdslm/Desktop/SOLIDER-REID/experiments/cargo_cvpb/afd_train.py:124), if only the target opposite-view prototype is initialized, masked CE becomes single-class CE ≈ 0. With `tau=0.05`, once noisy negatives appear, gradients can become sharp. Add logging for active OVP samples / valid prototype columns, and preferably warm up `ovp_lambda` or `tau`.
experiments/cargo_cvpb/codex_review_raw.txt:5170:- **High**: Novelty collision risk is real. CMPC already uses cross-modal prototypes, momentum memories, and contrastive loss pulling one modality toward the other modality’s prototype; this is very close structurally to “aerial sample -> ground pid prototype” InfoNCE. VI-ReID also has older center/prototype cross-modality alignment precedent. Treat OVP-Mem as an empirical auxiliary loss, not the main contribution.
experiments/cargo_cvpb/codex_review_raw.txt:5172:- **Medium**: The comment “OVP loss in fp32” is not guaranteed. The call is inside CUDA autocast at [afd_train.py](/Users/abdslm/Desktop/SOLIDER-REID/experiments/cargo_cvpb/afd_train.py:260), and the matmul in [afd_train.py](/Users/abdslm/Desktop/SOLIDER-REID/experiments/cargo_cvpb/afd_train.py:141) may autocast despite `bn.float()`. Not likely to overflow (`cos/tau` about `[-20,20]`), but wrap OVP loss in `autocast(enabled=False)` if fp32 is intended.
experiments/cargo_cvpb/codex_review_raw.txt:5174:- **Medium**: Header says update prototypes then apply loss, but implementation computes loss first and updates the bank after optimizer step at [afd_train.py](/Users/abdslm/Desktop/SOLIDER-REID/experiments/cargo_cvpb/afd_train.py:281). This makes OVP a one-iteration stale memory bank and prevents same-batch opposite-view evidence from helping cold-start. That may be acceptable, but docs/comments should match.
experiments/cargo_cvpb/codex_review_raw.txt:5176:- **Medium**: OVP averages losses by view group, not by sample count, in [afd_train.py](/Users/abdslm/Desktop/SOLIDER-REID/experiments/cargo_cvpb/afd_train.py:122). This gives A->G and G->A equal weight when both exist. Good for symmetry, but document it because λ is not a plain per-sample mean.
